The '''1.5.0.001''' (or '''1.5.0.1''') update, also known as the '''"Draedon Update"''', added [[The Codebreaker]] and a [[boss]] battle with the [[The Fabricational Quartet]] featuring Draedon. It also added [[Malice Mode]] along with several other game mechanics, and numerous items ranging from [[weapons]] to [[vanity items]]. The update additionally reworked many mechanics such as the [[Rage Meter]], changed core-vanilla Terraria mechanics such as [[Goblin Tinkerer]]'s reforge system and movement speed bonuses, and resprited a plethora of textures including a complete visual overhaul of [[Supreme Calamitas]] and the mod icon.

Thousands of boss changes, balance changes, fixes, and additional changes were also implemented. Notably, most Godseeker Mode stats from players and enemies were both significantly reduced to be less dramatic, and a new [[Configuration options|option]] to rework the Early Hardmode progression is introduced.
<!-- This changelog has taken 202 hours of my life. LOL - Merkalto -->

== Changes ==
=== Bosses ===
*All bosses that have ''Bullet Hell'' phases have had their projectile spawning code adjusted for smoother performance.
*All bosses except Desert Scourge have had their player targeting improved slightly.
*Boss Reactive Damage reduction is now removed if a boss has been defeated. <!-- unless the world had Malice active, this info goes on the malice page, not here -->
*In multiplayer, Jungle Dragon, Yharon and Providence, the Profaned Goddess can now target and fire projectiles at multiple players at once. This is capped at 5 players.
*If the player does less than 1 damage to a boss, the damage is no longer rounded up to 1 (2 on a critical hit) and is instead rounded down to 0.
*All bosses in Revengeance Mode or higher can now become more aggressive and enter their later phases regardless of their current HP if the player takes too long to defeat it. The timer for each boss is shared with its [[Reactive Damage Reduction]].
*All bosses that enrage outside of a specific biome now have a 5-second delay before enraging.
*All Calamity boss [[debuff]] immunities and inflictions have been overhauled. All bosses remain immune to [[Confused]] and [[Pearl Aura]]. A more detailed list can be found below:
{{collapse top|Boss Debuff Immunities (Click to open)|(Click to close)|}}
*Most boss debuff immunities have not been adjusted:
**Plantera is now immune to Venom.
**The Moon Lord is now immune to Nightwither. <!-- Exo Freeze, Glacial State, and Temporal Sadness don't work on normal/expert Queen Bee. -->
*All previous Calamity boss debuff immunities have been removed.
*Crabulon, Desert Scourge, The Dragonfolly, Hive Mind, Leviathan, The Perforators, and Ravager are no longer immune to any debuffs besides Confusion and Pearl Aura.
*The Slime God Core, Crimulan, and Ebonian Slime Gods are now immune to Poisoned.
**The Corrupt and Crimson slime spawns are also now immune to Poisoned.
*Cryogen is now immune to Glacial State and Exo Freeze.
**Aurora Spirits and Cryocores are also now immune to Glacial State and Exo Freeze.
*Aquatic Scourge is now immune to Irradiated and Sulphuric Poisoning.
*Brimstone Elemental and Brimlings are now immune to Abyssal Flames and Brimstone Flames.
*Calamitas, Cataclysm, and Catastrophe are now immune to Abyssal Flames and Brimstone Flames.
**Life and Soul Seekers are also now immune to Abyssal Flames and Brimstone Flames.
*Anahita is now immune to Glacial State and Exo Freeze.
*Astrum Aureus is now immune to Astral Infection and Poisoned.
**Aureus Spawns' are also now immune to Astral Infection and Poisoned.
*The Plaguebringer Goliath is now immune to Plague, Poisoned, and Venom. <!-- Also includes mines -->
**Plaguebringers are also now immune to Plague, Poisoned, and Venom.
*Astrum Deus is now immune to Kami Flu.
*Profaned Guardians are now immune to Holy Flames and Nightwither.
*Providence, the Profaned Goddess is now immune to Holy Flames and Nightwither.
*Storm Weaver is immune to all debuffs in phase 1.
*Storm Weaver is now immune to Electrified in phase 2.
*Ceaseless Void is immune to all debuffs.
**Dark Energies are also immune to all debuffs.
*Signus, Envoy of the Devourer is now immune to Whispering Death.
*Polterghast is now immune to Nightwither and Whispering Death.
*The Old Duke is now immune to Irradiated and Sulphuric Poisoning.
*The Devourer of Gods is immune to all debuffs.
*Jungle Dragon, Yharon is now immune to On Fire!
*Supreme Calamitas, Supreme Cataclysm, and Supreme Catastrophe and now immune to Abyssal Flames and Brimstone Flames.
**Supreme Soul Seekers are also now immune to Abyssal Flames and Brimstone Flames.
{{collapse bottom}}
{{collapse top|Boss Debuff Inflictions (Click to Open)|(Click to Close)|}}
*Astrum Deus mine projectiles will now inflict Astral Infection for 3 seconds instead of 5. Astral shot projectiles will now inflict Astral Infection for 2 seconds instead of 3.
*Aquatic Scourge will now only inflict Irradiated for 3 seconds and can no longer inflict Venom and Bleeding. <!-- Tail inflicts for 2 seconds. -->
**The Aquatic Scourge head will inflict Irradiated for 6 seconds.
**Aquatic Parasite will now inflict Irradiated for 2 seconds instead of Venom.
**Aquatic Urchin will now inflict Irradiated for 2 seconds instead of Venom.
*Brimstone Elemental Brimstone Hellfireball projectiles will now inflict Brimstone Flames for 2 seconds instead of 4.
*Calamitas will now inflict Brimstone Flames for 3 seconds instead of 5.
**Cataclysm will now inflict Brimstone Flames for 3 seconds instead of 5.
**Catastrophe will now inflict Brimstone Flames for 3 seconds instead of 5.
**Life Seekers can no longer inflict any debuffs.
*The Devourer of Gods can no longer inflict Frostburn.
*Leviathan will now inflict Bleeding for 4 seconds instead of Wet.
*Anahita will no longer inflict any debuffs.
**Aquatic Aberrations will now inflict Bleeding for 2 seconds instead of Wet.
**Paraseas will now inflict Bleeding for 2 seconds instead of Wet.
*Lunatic Cultist Ice Mists will now inflict Frozen for 1.5 seconds and Chilled for 3 seconds instead of 1.5 seconds of Frozen for 1, Chilled for 2 seconds. Ice Mists can no longer inflict Frostburn. <!-- Also affects adult Eidolon Wyrms, Eidolon Wyrms, and Eidolists -->
*The Old Duke will now inflict Irradiated for 8 seconds and can no longer inflict Bleeding, Feral Bite, and Poisoned. Old Duke toxic cloud projectiles will now inflict Irradiated for 4 seconds instead of inflicting Venom for 5 seconds.
**Old Duke Tooth Balls will now inflict Irradiated for 4 seconds and can no longer inflict Bleeding, Feral Bite, and Poisoned.
**Sulphurous Sharkrons will now inflict Irradiated for 4 seconds and can no longer inflict Bleeding, Feral Bite, and Poisoned.
*The Perforator Hive will now inflict Burning Blood for 3 seconds.
*The Perforator Worms can no longer inflict Bleeding. <!-- Not to be confused with Burning Blood. -->
*The Plaguebringer Goliath will now inflict Plague for 4 seconds instead of 5. Plaguebringer Goliath Plague Missiles will now inflict Plague for 3 seconds instead of 5.
*Plantera will now only inflict Poisoned for 5 seconds.
**Plantera Tentacles will now only inflict Poisoned for 3 seconds.
*Polterghast Phantom Shot projectiles will now inflict Whispering Death for 2 seconds on hit. Phantom Hook Shot projectiles will now inflict Nightwither for 2 seconds on hit.
*Profaned Guardians will now inflict Holy Flames for 4 seconds instead of 5.
*Profaned Guardian (Crystal) will now inflict Holy Flames for 3 seconds instead of 5.
*Defense Profaned Guardian will now inflict Holy Flames for 4 seconds instead of 5.
*Healer Profaned Guardian will no longer inflict any debuffs.
*Providence, the Profaned Goddess's projectiles now inflict Holy Flames for 3 seconds instead of 2-5. Holy Ray beam now inflicts Holy Flames for 7 seconds instead of 5.
*Signus, Envoy of the Devourer's scythe projectiles now inflict Whispering Death for 3 seconds instead of 5.
*The Ebonian Slime God will now inflict Weak for 5 seconds instead of 3. <!-- 2 seconds when split -->
**Purple Abyss Mines inflict Weak for 0.75 seconds.
*The Crimulan Slime God will now inflict Darkness for 2 seconds instead of 1.5. <!-- 0.75 seconds when split -->
**Red Abyss Mines inflict Darkness for 4 seconds.
**Crimson Slime Spawns will now inflict Darkness for 1.5 seconds instead of 1.
**Spiked Crimson Slimes will now inflict Darkness for 1.5 seconds instead of 1. <!-- inflicts on contact and from spikes -->
*Storm Weaver Armored Body will now inflict Electrified for 1.5 seconds.
*Storm Weaver Armored Tail will now inflict Electrified for 1 second.
*Storm Weaver Body will now inflict Electrified for 1.5 seconds.
*Storm Weaver Head will now inflict Electrified for 3 seconds instead of 1.5 in Revengeance Mode.
*Storm Weaver Tail will now inflict Electrified for 1 second.
{{collapse bottom}}
*All debuffs that slow or freeze enemies will grant bosses a 30-second global cooldown before they can be slowed again. <!-- Affects: Vaporfied, Vulnerability Hex (INCOMPLETE) -->
*If the <code>Early Hardmode Progression Rework</code> is enabled, the Wall of Flesh and Mechanical bosses' stats and progression unlocks will be dramatically altered:
{{collapse top|Early Hardmode Progression Rework boss changes (Click to open|(Click to collapse)|}}
*Defeating Wall of Flesh will now cause Cobalt and Palladium to be spawned in the world and will allow any of the Mechanical Bosses to randomly spawn in the world. These effects no longer occur when Demon/Crimson Altars are destroyed.
*Crimson and Demon Altars will now drop 6 Souls of Night when broken.
*The Pwnhammer and Hammush will now state that Crimson and Demon altars will not spawn ores when broken.
*[[The Twins]], [[The Destroyer]], and [[Skeletron Prime]] now have dynamic stats and change depending on which Mechanical bosses the player defeats first.
**If no Mechanical Bosses have been defeated:
***The Mechanical Boss' base HP is decreased by 20%
***The Mechanical Boss' base damage is decreased by 20%
**If one Mechanical Boss has been defeated:
***The Mechanical Boss' base HP is decreased by 10%
***The Mechanical Boss' base damage is decreased by 10%
**If two Mechanical Bosses have been defeated:
***The Mechanical Boss has no stat adjustments.
*The first mechanical boss defeated will now cause Mythril and Orichalcum to be spawned in the world.
*The second mechanical boss defeated will now cause Adamantite and Titanium to be spawned in the world.
*The third mechanical boss defeated will now cause Hallowed Ore to be spawned in the underground Hallow Biome.
*All mechanical bosses will drop Hallowed Bars once they have all been defeated.
{{collapse bottom}}
*All damage taken by segmented bosses, Brimstone Hearts, and Dark Energies that can pierce have diminishing returns.
*All segmented bosses now resist damage dealt by True Melee strikes by 50%.
*All Vanilla bosses now utilize a standard {{dv|25%|33%}} drop chance for their regular loot. All Vanilla bosses no longer have mutually exclusive drops.

==== [[Anahita and Leviathan]] ====
*The Leviathan now utilizes 3 separate hitboxes for its head, tail, and body. All three hitboxes share the same immunity frame timer.
*Added a custom spawn animation for The Leviathan.

==== [[Adult Eidolon Wyrm]] ====
*Now classified as a [[Superboss]].
*Increased HP from {{dv|1,000,000|2,000,000}} to {{dv|2,100,000|3,360,000|3,864,000}}.
*Decreased head defense from 3000 to 700 and DR from 99.99999% to 40%.
*Adult Eidolon Wyrm AI has been completely overhauled:
**Now has two phases, each phase will consist of several attacks.
**Will always chase and occasionally lunge towards the player.
**In phase 1:
***After three lunges have been performed lightning projectiles will be rained down on the player.
***After four lunges are performed, it will spawn in a [[Eidolon Wyrm]].
***After seven lunges are performed, it will fire three barrages of Ice Mist projectiles from below the player.
***After eight lunges are performed, it will rapidly dash into the player and spawn in five [[Eidolist]]s.
***After nine lunges the counter is reset.
**At 50% HP, it will enter phase 2:
**After three lunges are performed, it will spin around the player's last position and will consecutively fire shadowflame fireballs towards the center. If the player is not being spun around, these projectiles travel 50% faster.
**After four lunges are performed, lightning projectiles will be rained down on the player.
***After seven lunges are performed, it will spawn seven Ancient blasts that will circle the player's last position and explode three times. Each time an ancient blast projectile explodes, it releases four ancient blast shards.
***After nine lunges are performed, it will again fire three barrages of Ice Mist projectiles from below the player. After nine lunges the counter is reset.

*AI is now affected by difficulty changes.

==== [[Aquatic Scourge]] ====
*Increased HP from {{dv|73,000|116,800|136,000}} to {{dv|80,000|128,000|147,200}}.
*Increased body segment DR from 5% to 10%.
*Decreased head DR from 10% to 5%.
*No longer enrages if the player flips gravity.
*Increased its out of biome enrage velocity by 40%.
*Now only uses its projectile vomit attack when it has 75% HP or less.
*Now only releases toxic clouds when it has 50% HP or less. Clouds now spawn at a consistent rate.
*Toxic cloud projectiles now use a circular hitbox.
*Now telegraphs its Revengeance Mode spin attack by turning brownish over time. If it is submerged in liquid it will return to its normal color hue and will not perform its spin attack.
*Reactive DR effectiveness is now increased by 100% if Plantera has not been defeated.

==== [[Astrum Aureus]] ====
*Decreased defense from 50 to 40.
*Decreased post-Moon Lord HP from {{revengeance|624,000}} to {{dv|230,000|368,000|468,000}}.
*Post-Moon Lord buff is no longer exclusive to Revengeance Mode.
*Increased Aureus Spawn post-Moon Lord HP from 3000 to 8250.
*Decreased Astrum Aureus and Aureus Spawn post-Moon Lord damage multipliers from 3x to 2x.
*Will now always jump towards a player instead of away from them.
*Can now walk on platforms in its idle phase.
*Decreased recharge timer from 2.5 seconds to 2. While enraged, it will no longer recharge.
*Now only fires 6 crystals instead of 10 while it has 75% HP or more.
**Crystals are no longer fired at the same time, they are now fired consecutively.
*Now has a third phase that starts at 50% HP, during this phase, it now cycles through all of its attacks 50% faster. Its recharge timer does not change. <!-- Astrum Aureus' teleport is no longer counted as a separate attack. -->
*Its hitbox has been split into three ovals to make the fight fairer.
*Now drops {{gc|25}} instead of {{gc|35}}.

==== [[Astrum Deus]] ====
*Increased HP from {{dv|187,500|300,000|360,000}} to {{dv|200,000|320,000|384,000}}.
*No longer enrages if the player flips gravity.
*Summoning animation is now sped up by 100% when using Starcore.
*Astral Mine projectiles now use a circular hitbox.
*Initial invulnerability in the second phase is reduced from 10 seconds to 5.
*Second phase clone now has a distinct glowmask.
*Reactive DR effectiveness is now increased by 100% if the Moon Lord has not been defeated.
*Now resists the [[Tenebreus Tides]] water blade projectiles by 50%.
*No longer resists the [[Stardust Dragon Staff]] minion by 75%, and the [[Lazhar]], [[Inferno Fork]], [[Cosmic Rainbow]], [[Plague Staff]], [[Eidolon Staff]], and [[Charged Blaster Cannon]] by 25%.
*Now drops {{gc|25}} instead of {{gc|20}}.

==== [[Brimstone Elemental]] ====

*Decreased cocoon defense from 99,999 to 60.
*Decreased ray phase defense from 45 to 30.
*Decreased Brimling cocoon DR from 80% to 60%.
*Can no longer fire projectiles as her cocoon phase starts.
*Brimstone Dart rings from her cocoon phase now rotate every other shot.
*Her inability to teleport below 33% health is now exclusive to Revengeance and Death Mode.
*Can no longer go into her cocoon unless she has performed 3 attacks.

==== [[Calamitas]] ====
*No longer has a first phase.<!-- It now uses its old first phase HP as its new total HP - Merkalto -->

*Decreased Catastrophe post-Providence HP from {{dv|22,500|45,000|66,150}} to {{dv|15,000|30,000|44,100}}.
*Calamitas' overall movement is now more fluid.
*Now utilizes the same position tracker that Supreme Calamitas uses.
*Will now only dash below 75% HP.
*Dashes no longer predict the player's movement.
*At 70% HP and at 10% HP, will now initiate a bullet-hell. During this time Calamitas will be invulnerable and will hover above the player.
**Bullet-hells will last 15 seconds.
*At 40% HP, will spawn both of her brothers. No longer spawns her brothers periodically throughout the fight.
*Soul Seekers can no longer fire while Calamitas is firing a barrage of lasers.
*Increased reactive DR duration from 190 seconds to 240 seconds.

==== [[Ceaseless Void]] ====
*Increased HP from {{dv|200|400}} to {{dv|67,200|107,520|123,648}}.
*Decreased DR from 99.99% to 50%.
*Decreased Dark Energy HP from {{dv|24,000|48,000}} to {{dv|6,000|12,000}}.
*Dark Energy now resists damage from the [[Nuclear Fury]] and [[Wave Pounder]] by 50%.
*Dark Energies will now spawn in clusters of 3-5 and revolve around Ceaseless Void. When the total HP of all of the Dark Energies is at 50% (1st time), 35% (2nd time), 25% (3rd time), or 20% (4th time), all Dark Energies will become invulnerable and all Dark Energies and players will be sucked towards Ceaseless Void. During this time Ceaseless Void can be damaged.
*When Ceaseless Void is at 5% HP, it will suck in all Dark Energies and players for 15 seconds. After the 15 seconds have ended, Ceaseless Void will implode and drop its loot.
*Removed the tile enrage mechanic.
*Now drops its own Treasure Bag in Expert Mode or higher.

==== [[The Devourer of Gods]] ====
*Now uses one entity to make up its two phases. The Devourer of Gods will transition into its second phase at 60% HP. During this time its health bar will vanish.
*Decreased HP from {{dv|1,825,000|2,920,000|3,360,000}} to {{dv|888,750|1,422,000|1,706,400}}.

*Increased phase 2 head damage from {{dv|300|600|650}} to {{dv|350|700|750|780}}.
*Decreased Cosmic Guardian HP from {{dv|100,000|200,000}} to {{dv|50,000|100,000}}.
*Background shader is no longer present while fighting the weakened Sentinels.
*In both phases, The Devourer of Gods will now fire out a single laser directly towards the player during its laser barrages.
*Added a custom portal animation that plays while The Devourer of Gods is teleporting. This effect also occurs when transitioning into its second phase.
*Phase 2 will now only teleport when below 30% HP.
*Devourer of Gods will now unleash a barrage of cosmic fireballs when it teleports.
*Defeating The Devourer of Gods' second phase will now enable the buffed Solar Eclipse event.
*In both phases, the head and tail segments now take less damage the further away they are from the player. The maximum damage reduction is 25%.
*Head segment in both phases now take 10% increased damage instead of 25%. The tail segment in both phases still takes 25% increased damage.
*Body segments no longer have increased DR in Death Mode, and instead have a static 92.5% DR.
*Body segments no longer have Reactive DR.
*Now resists the [[Valediction]] stealth strike by 10%, [[Dark Spark]] by 15%, Wave Pounder by 25%, and the [[Sealed Singularity]] by 50%.
*Now takes 15% more damage from the [[Time Bolt]] stealth strike.
*Now drops {{gc|80}} instead of {{pc|1}}. <!-- First phase no longer drops money -->

==== [[The Twins]] ====
*No longer enrage if the player flips gravity.
*Retinazer now slows down between 25% and 50% while using a true melee weapon depending on how close the player is to it.
*Decreased Retinazer Revengeance Mode HP from {{revengeance|44,850}} to {{revengeance|39,000}}.
*Increased Spazmatism Revengeance Mode HP from {{revengeance|37,500}} to {{revengeance|46,575}}.
*Decreased Retinazer phase 2 defense in Revengeance Mode from {{revengeance|30}} to {{revengeance|20}}.
*Decreased Spazmatism phase 2 defense in Revengeance Mode from {{revengeance|40}} to {{revengeance|28}}.

==== [[Wall of Flesh]] ====
*Will now shoot out a demon scythe when releasing Leeches in Revengeance and Death Mode.

=== Balance ===
*All items that deal damage now utilize a '''{{color|+5%|green}}/{{color|-5%|red}}''' damage deviation instead of vanilla Terraria's base '''{{color|+15%|green}}/{{color|-15%|red}}''' damage deviations.
*All bonus projectiles spawned by coverings and armor sets can no longer proc themselves.
*All items, buffs, and debuffs that grant the player bonus immunity frames are now additive.
*All defense increasing modifiers now scale throughout progression:
**The '''Hard''' modifier now increase defense by {{color|+1|green}} / {{color|+2|green}} once Golem or Moon Lord has been defeated / {{color|+3|green}} once Providence has been defeated / {{color|+4|green}} once The Devourer of Gods has been defeated.
**The '''Guarding''' modifier now increases defense by {{color|+2|green}} / {{color|+3|green}} once Wall of Flesh has been defeated / {{color|+4|green}} once Golem has been defeated / {{color|+5|green}} once Moon Lord has been defeated / {{color|+6|green}} once Providence has been defeated / {{color|+7|green}} once Polterghast has been defeated / {{color|+8|green}} once The Devourer of Gods has been defeated.
**The '''Armored''' modifier now increases defense by {{color|+3|green}} / {{color|+4|green}} once Wall of Flesh has been defeated / {{color|+6|green}} once Golem has been defeated / {{color|+7|green}} once Moon Lord has been defeated / {{color|+9|green}} once Providence has been defeated / {{color|+10|green}} once Polterghast has been defeated / {{color|+12|green}} once The Devourer of Gods has been defeated.
**The '''Warding''' modifier now increases defense by {{color|+4|green}} / {{color|+6|green}} once Wall of Flesh has been defeated / {{color|+8|green}} once Golem has been defeated / {{color|+10|green}} once Moon Lord has been defeated / {{color|+12|green}} once Providence has been defeated / {{color|+14|green}} once Polterghast has been defeated / {{color|+16|green}} once The Devourer of Gods has been defeated.
*The critical strike chance modifiers have had their nerfs reverted:
**The '''Precise''' modifier now grants {{color|+2%|green}} critical strike chance.
**The '''Lucky''' modifier now grants {{color|+4%|green}} critical strike chance.
*Nerfed several [[Proficiency]] bonuses. A detailed list can be found below:
{{collapse top|Proficiency stat adjustments (Click to Open|(Click to Close)}}
 <!-- All of these values were updated on the not-so-dynamic tooltips for the level meters. - Merkalto -->
*Decreased level 4 Melee Proficiency melee damage bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 5 Melee Proficiency melee swing speed bonus from 2% to 1%.
*Decreased level 6 Melee Proficiency melee swing speed bonus from 3% to 1%.
*Decreased level 7 Melee Proficiency melee swing speed bonus from 4% to 2% and melee critical strike chance bonus from 2% to 1%.
*Decreased level 8 Melee Proficiency melee damage bonus from 5% to 4% and melee swing speed bonus from 5% to 2%.
*Decreased level 9 Melee Proficiency melee swing speed bonus from 5% to 2% and melee critical strike chance bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 10 Melee Proficiency melee damage bonus from 6% to 5%, melee swing speed bonus from 6% to 3%, and melee critical strike chance bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 11 Melee Proficiency melee damage bonus from 7% to 5%, melee swing speed bonus from 7% to 2%, and melee critical strike chance bonus from 4% to 2%.
*Decreased level 12 Melee Proficiency melee damage bonus from 8% to 5%, melee swing speed bonus from 8% to 4%, and melee critical strike chance bonus from 4% to 3%.

*Decreased level 14 Melee Proficiency melee damage bonus from 10% to 6%, melee swing speed bonus from 10% to 5%, and melee critical strike chance bonus from 5% to 3%.
*Decreased level 15 Melee Proficiency melee damage bonus from 12% to 6%, melee swing speed bonus from 12% to 6%, and melee critical strike chance bonus from 6% to 3%.
*Decreased level 4 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 5 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 3% to 2%. Increased its movement speed bonus from 1% to 2%.
*Decreased level 6 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 4% to 2%. Increased its movement speed bonus from 1% to 3%.
*Decreased level 7 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 4% to 2%. Increased its movement speed bonus from 2% to 3%.
*Decreased level 8 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 5% to 3%. Increased its movement speed bonus from 2% to 3%.
*Decreased level 9 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 5% to 3% and ranged critical strike chance bonus from 3% to 2%. Increased its movement speed bonus from 2% to 4%.
*Decreased level 10 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 6% to 4% and ranged critical strike chance bonus from 3% to 2%. Increased its movement speed bonus from 3% to 4%.
*Decreased level 11 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 7% to 4% and ranged critical strike chance bonus from 4% to 3%. Increased its movement speed bonus from 3% to 4%.
*Decreased level 12 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 8% to 4% and ranged critical strike chance bonus from 4% to 3%. Increased its movement speed bonus from 4% to 5%.
*Decreased level 13 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 9% to 5% and ranged critical strike chance bonus from 5% to 3%. Increased its movement speed bonus from 4% to 5%.
*Decreased level 14 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 10% to 5% and ranged critical strike chance bonus from 5% to 3%. Increased its movement speed bonus from 5% to 6%.
*Decreased level 15 Ranged Proficiency ranged damage bonus from 12% to 6% and ranged critical strike chance bonus from 6% to 3%.
*Decreased level 4 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 5 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 6 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 4% to 3% and mana cost reduction bonus from 4% to 2%.

*Decreased level 8 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 5% to 3% and mana cost reduction bonus from 5% to 3%.
*Decreased level 9 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 5% to 4%, mana cost reduction bonus from 5% to 3%, and magic critical strike chance bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 10 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 6% to 4%, mana cost reduction bonus from 6% to 4%, and magic critical strike chance bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 11 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 7% to 4%, mana cost reduction bonus from 7% to 4%, and magic critical strike chance bonus from 4% to 3%.
*Decreased level 12 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 8% to 4%, mana cost reduction bonus from 8% to 5%, and magic critical strike chance bonus from 4% to 3%.
*Decreased level 13 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 9% to 5%, mana cost reduction bonus from 9% to 5%, and magic critical strike chance bonus from 5% to 3%.
*Decreased level 14 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 10% to 5%, mana cost reduction bonus from 10% to 6%, and magic critical strike chance bonus from 5% to 3%.
*Decreased level 15 Magic Proficiency magic damage bonus from 12% to 6%, mana cost reduction bonus from 12% to 6%, and magic critical strike chance bonus from 6% to 3%.
*Summoner Proficiency no longer grants minion slots.
*Increased level 10 Summoner Proficiency summon damage bonus from 6% to 7%.
*Decreased level 4 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 5 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 6 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 4% to 2%.
*Decreased level 7 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 4% to 3%, Spy projectile velocity bonus from 4% to 3%, and Spy critical strike chance bonus from 2% to 1%.
*Decreased level 8 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 5% to 3% and Spy projectile velocity bonus from 5% to 3%.
*Decreased level 9 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 5% to 3%, Spy projectile velocity bonus from 5% to 4%, and Spy critical strike chance bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 10 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 6% to 4%, Spy projectile velocity bonus from 6% to 4%, and Spy critical strike chance bonus from 3% to 2%.
*Decreased level 11 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 7% to 4%, Spy projectile velocity bonus from 7% to 4%, and Spy critical strike chance bonus from 4% to 3%.

*Decreased level 13 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 9% to 5%, Spy projectile velocity bonus from 9% to 5%, and Spy critical strike chance bonus from 5% to 3%.
*Decreased level 14 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 10% to 5%, Spy projectile velocity bonus from 10% to 6%, and Spy critical strike chance bonus from 5% to 3%.
*Decreased level 15 Spy Proficiency Spy damage bonus from 12% to 6%, Spy projectile velocity bonus from 12% to 6%, and Spy critical strike chance bonus from 6% to 3%.
{{collapse bottom}}
*The Mana regeneration delay has been dramatically decreased. <!-- 0.525 to 0.067 when mana near full & 3.325 to 0.867 when mana is empty -->
*Spy Stealth damage calculations have been overhauled and are now determined based on how long the player's stealth takes to charge, the weapon's use-time, and the weapon's damage. All stealth strike damage is calculated first using Spy stealth, then any additional damage adjustments are made on a projectile basis. Stealth strike minimum damage bonus is +100% damage (+200% damage in total) and the maximum damage bonus is +1000% (+1100% in total). Spy stealth no longer boosts the player's movement speed or critical strike chance.
**Additionally all Spy stealth damage is now listed as a separate stat on Spy weapons.

==== Armor and coverings ====
<!-- Most Movement Speed balance changes are not listed here, they are in a collapsible table under Reworks. - Merkalto -->
*[[Abaddon]], [[Asgardian Aegis]], and [[Asgard's Valor]] now grant immunity to the [[Searing Lava]] debuff.
*[[The Absorber]], [[Grand Gelatin]], [[Heart of the Elements]], [[The Sponge]], and the [[Statigel armor]] set bonus jump speed boosts are now 75% less effective if the player has auto jump. This is now reflected in their tooltips.
**Additionally, decreased Grand Gelatin jump speed bonus from 40% to 24%.
*Buffed The Absorber, [[Fungal Carapace]], and The Sponge mushroom spore damage from 56 to 70, and spore lifespan from 2 seconds to 5.
*Buffed The Absorber, Grand Gelatin, Life Jelly, and The Sponge life regeneration boost while standing still from 2 to 4.
*Buffed The Absorber, Grand Gelatin, Mana Jelly, and The Sponge mana regeneration boost while standing still from 2 to 4.
*[[The Absorber]] and [[The Sponge]] no longer inherit the effect of [[Amidias' Spark]] and [[Giant Shell]]. <!-- Recipe changes -->
*Buffed The Absorber's damage reduction bonus from 5% to 10% and its defense from 6 to 10.
*[[Abyssal Amulet]] and [[Lumenous Amulet]] now grant immunity to the [[Crush Depth]] debuff.
*[[Aero Stone]] no longer boosts all damage by 3% and now grants 10% bonus flight time.
*The [[Aerospec Helm]], [[Daedalus Helm]], [[God Slayer Horned Greathelm]], [[Hydrothermic Helm]], [[Statigel Helm]], [[Tarragon Helm]], [[Victide Helm]], and [[Wulfrum Helm]] set bonuses now all increase the player's aggro.
*The [[Aerospec Helmet]], [[Astral Breastplate]], [[Auric Tesla Space Helmet]], [[Daedalus Mask]], [[Demonshade Helm]], [[Fathom Swarmer Breastplate]], [[Fearmonger armor]] and Plate Mail, [[Forbidden Circlet]], [[Hydrothermic Helmet]], [[Plaguebringer Visor]] and Carapace, [[Silva Horned Hood]], [[Statigel Hood]], [[Tarragon Horned Helm]], [[Victide Helmet]], and [[Wulfrum Helmet]] minion slot bonuses have been moved to their corresponding set bonuses.
*Nerfed [[Affliction]]'s defense from 20 to 10 and its buff no longer displays it at 1 second in the icon list.
*[[Alchemical Flask]] no longer reduces the damage or removes the blindness of the Plague debuff. Plague seekers now have a base damage of 30 and no longer have a damage softcap.
*[[The Amalgam]] no longer inherits effects from the [[Fungal Clump]], [[Leviathan Ambergris]], and [[Void of Extinction]]. <!-- These items are removed from the recipe -->
*Buffed The Amalgam and [[Amalgamated Brain]]'s base debuff duration from 1.5-5 seconds to 5-8 seconds.
*[[Ambrosial Ampoule]] no longer grants mining speed bonus, immunity to poison and venom, resistance to bee attacks, and honey-like life regeneration. Buffed damage reduction boost from 5% to 7%, defense boost from 4 to 6, and passive life regeneration from 2 to 4.
*Nerfed [[Angel Treads]] wing flight time bonus from 20% to 10%.
*Nerfed [[Angel Wings]] defense bonus from 15 to 10 and life regeneration bonus from 3 to 2.
*The [[Ankh Shield]] and its upgrades now grant immunity to [[Mighty Wind]].
*Adjusted [[Archaic Powder]]'s boosts in the [[Underground]], [[Cavern]] or [[Underworld]] layers from 7% damage reduction and 3 defense to 5% damage reduction and 10 defense.
*[[Artemis Emblem]] and [[Elemental Quiver]] ammo reduction bonuses can now be properly stacked with each other and with the [[Chlorophyte armor|Chlorophyte Helmet]].
*[[Asgard's Valor]], [[Asgardian Aegis]], and [[Elysian Aegis]] shield bash geysers can now only hit an enemy 6 times per second instead of 30 times.
*Asgard's Valor, Asgardian Aegis, and Elysian Aegis now grant the player 6 immunity frames instead of 4 when a shield bash is performed.
**All shield bashes no longer grant immunity frames if the player is already immune.
*Buffed Asgardian Aegis' dash length from 27 blocks to 28 and it now grants 1 life regeneration while equipped. Nerfed explosion damage from 300 to 255 and holy eruption damage from 225 to 150.
*Buffed [[Astral Arcanum]]'s life regeneration granted while affected by an alcohol debuff from 1 to 2.
*[[Auric Tesla armor]] stats adjusted:
**[[Auric Tesla Royal Helm]] set bonus no longer inflicts Silva Stun on melee hits. Melee damage no longer scales with player health. <!-- I don't think anything inflicts Silva Stun anymore, idk - Merkalto -->
**[[Auric Tesla Plumed Helm]] set bonus no longer boosts Spy critical damage by 1.5x while above 50% HP.
**[[Auric Tesla Space Helmet]] set bonus now only grants 6 minion slots instead of 8. Moved 15% of its total summon damage bonus to the headpiece itself.
**Increased [[Auric Tesla Wire-Hemmed Visage]] magic damage bonus from 20% to 30%.
*[[Badge of Bravery]] no longer requires the [[Tarragon armor]] to receive its melee damage, critical strike chance, and armor penetration bonuses. Decreased its melee damage bonus from 10% to 5%.
*Nerfed [[Bat Wings]] damage bonus from 15% to 7% and critical strike chance bonus from 5% to 3%. It no longer grants any bonus defense.
*Nerfed [[Beetle Scale Mail]] set bonus [[Beetle Might]] buff, each stack of Beetle Might no longer grants additional melee swing speed.
*Nerfed [[Beetle Wings]]:
**[[Beetle Shell]] - Decreased Beetle Wings set bonus defense bonus from 15 to 10, and melee damage bonus from 10% to 5%.
**[[Beetle Scale Mail]] - Decreased Beetle Wings set bonus melee damage bonus from 10% to 5%.
*Decreased [[Betsy's Wings]], [[Nebula Mantle]], and [[Vortex Booster]] horizontal hover speeds by 10%.
*Nerfed [[Blazing Core]] sun damage from 1690 to 1270.
*[[Bloodflare armor]] stats adjusted:
**Bloodflare armor base set bonus can no longer cause Mana Stars to drop from enemies that are above 50% HP. Increased the cooldown for [[Heart]]s to drop from enemies below 50% HP from 3 seconds to 5. Both changes are inherited by the Auric Tesla armor base set bonus.
*Decreased [[Bloodflare Horned Helm]] set bonus bloodflare soul projectile damage from 800 to 300 and they now grant enemies 10 local immunity frames instead of 2.
*Decreased [[Bloodflare Ram Mask]] set bonus melee hits lifesteal from 1-3 HP to 1-2 HP.
*Decreased [[Bloodflare Wyvern Helm]] set bonus phantom mine damage from 5000 to 3500. Moved 5% of its total summon damage bonus to the headpiece itself.
*Bloom Stone no longer increases damage and damages nearby enemies. It now increases life regeneration while grounded, which is more effective during the day.
*Buffed [[Blunder Booster]] and [[Plagued Fuel Pack]], decreased internal cooldown from 1.5 seconds to 1. While equipped, Stealth regeneration is now increased by 10%.
*Nerfed [[Bone Wings]] ranged damage bonus from 12% to 10%, and ranged critical strike chance bonus from 16% to 10%.
*[[Brimflame armor]] stats adjusted:
**Dereased Brimflame armor set bonus frenzy damage modifier from 150% to 130%.
**Increased Brimflame Cowl defense from 7 to 11.
**Increased Brimflame Robes defense from 10 to 15.
**Increased Brimflame Boots defense from 10 to 15.
*Nerfed [[Butterfly Wings]] mana bonus from 50 to 20 and magic damage bonus from 10% to 5%.
*[[The Camper]] now causes the player to deal 90% less damage while moving instead of 0 damage. Removed its +2 life regeneration bonus.
*[[Celestial Tracers]], [[Elysian Tracers]], and [[Seraph Tracers]] no longer gain additional flight time inherited from Rocket Boots.
*Nerfed Celestial Tracers flight time from 280 to 250 and top movement speed from 61 mph to 54 mph.
*Nerfed [[Celestial Jewel]] defense granted under the effects of a damaging debuff from 20 to 15. Nerfed life regen bonus from 3 to 2, and cumulatively 6 to 4 under the effects of a damaging debuff.
*Decreased [[Corrosive Spine]] projectile count per-hit from 3-6 to 2-4 and its projectiles now properly deal Spy damage. Additionally, the projectiles now have a base damage of 35 instead of dealing 70% of the weapon's damage.
*Increased [[Counter Scarf]] dash length from 13 blocks to 14. However, it no longer grants the '''Scarf Boosts''' buff upon proccing a dash. Decreased its true melee damage bonus from 15% to 10%.
*Buffed [[Crown Jewel]] life regeneration bonus from 1 to 2. Increased the life regeneration bonus while under the effects of a damaging debuff from 2 to 4.
*Nerfed [[Dark Sun Ring]] defense bonus during nighttime and Solar Eclipses from 30 to 15.
*[[Deep Diver]] now grants 15 defense while submerged in liquids instead of granting 15% increased defense. Increased its base defense boost from 2 to 8.
*[[Deific Amulet]] and [[Rampart of Deities]] no longer grant armor penetration bonuses. They also no longer inherit the effects of [[Jellyfish Necklace]] and [[Panic Necklace]]. <!-- Three necklaces removed from Deific -->
*Nerfed [[Demon Wings]] damage bonus from 10% to 5%.
*[[Desert Prowler armor]]'s tornado no longer has a damage softcap.
*[[Draedon's Heart]] now grants an additional 75% defense and 50% resistance to both damage and defense damage while standing still. It no longer inherits the effect of Heart of Darkness, Laudanum, or Stress Pills. <!-- Due to being moved to the The Fabricational Quartet, it also got its flavor tooltip back -->
*[[Dragon Scales]] infernado projectile now lasts for 14 seconds instead of 7. Nerfed infernado base damage from 840 to 550, its projectile size is no longer scaled up, and it now grants enemies 10 ID static immunity frames instead of 1 local immunity frame. <!-- Fun Fact: During testing this covering spawned two infernadoes at once, it doesn't do that anymore... - Merkalto -->
*Dragon Scales, [[Ethereal Extorter]], [[Feather Crown]], [[Moonstone Crown]], [[Nanotech]], and [[Scuttler's Jewel]] bonus projectiles now all deal Spy damage and have base damage values. <!-- scale fireballs = 80, nano = 120, moon crown = 85, extorter soul = 25, jewel = 15, feather crown = 15. -->
*[[Dynamo Stem Cells]] now has a static 20% chance to fire swarmer projectiles from ranged weapons instead of a random chance between 1% and 80%.
*Nerfed [[Eclipse Mirror]] darksun blast damage from 7000 to 2750.
*[[Electrician's Glove]] spark projectiles now deal Spy damage and have a base damage of 20. Decreased its stealth strike armor penetration bonus from 30 to 20.
*[[Elemental Gauntlet]] and [[Yharim's Insignia]] no longer increase the player's immunity frames after being hit.
*Elemental Gauntlet no longer has a 20% chance to inflict the Glacial State debuff onto enemies.
*Elemental Quiver no longer causes ranged projectiles to split.
*Nerfed Elysian Aegis dash length from 29 blocks to 28 and maximum bonus HP from 40 to 30. It no longer grants 2 life regeneration. Decreased explosion damage from 210 to 160 and holy eruption damage from 140 to 105.
**[[Elysian Guard]] now grants 40 defense instead of 20.
*Buffed [[Elysian Wings]] flight time from 200 to 240.
*Nerfed Elysian Tracers flight time from 200 to 180 and top movement speed from 53 mph to 49 mph.
*[[Ethereal Talisman]] and [[Sigil of Calamitas]] no longer restore mana when damaged or reveal treasure locations.
*[[Evasion Scarf]] no longer grants the '''Evasion Scarf Boost''' buff upon proccing a dash. Decreased true melee damage bonus modifier from 25% to 15%.
*Buffed [[Exodus Wings]] horizontal speed from 8.5 to 9 and acceleration multiplier from 2 to 2.5.
*Nerfed [[Fairy Wings]] maximum bonus HP from 80 to 60.
*Nerfed Fathom Swarmer Breastplate damage reduction bonus from 12% to 6%.
*Fearmonger armor stats adjusted:
**Decreased Fearmonger armor set bonus total minion slots granted from 6 to 2 and the set bonus minion damage boost from 30% to 20%.
**Fearmonger armor set bonus life regeneration now lasts for 1.5 seconds instead of 3, and decreased the life regeneration boost from 14 to 7.
*[[Feather Crown]] and Moonstone Crown now spawn in 3 bonus projectiles instead of 6 when a stealth strike is performed.
*Nerfed [[Festive Wings]] maximum bonus HP from 60 to 50.
*Nerfed [[Flame Wings]] melee damage bonus from 10% to 5%.
*[[Frost Barrier]] will no longer inflict Glacial State when hit while any bosses are alive. This change is inherited by Auric Tesla armor.
*Buffed Fungal Carapace defense boost from 2 to 6.
*[[Fungal Symbiote]] mushroom projectiles no longer have a damage cap. However, the mushroom projectiles now deal 15% of the weapon's damage instead of 25%.
*Buffed [[Giant Tortoise Shell]] defense from 8 to 12.
*Nerfed [[Glove of Recklessness]] Spy attack speed bonus from 20% to 12%.
*[[Gravistar Sabaton]] explosions now deal Spy damage. Nerfed explosion base damage from 300 to 50.
*[[Harpy Wings]] + Harpy Ring/Angel Treads set bonus feather projectile no longer has a soft damage cap and instead deals 30% of the weapon's damage.
*Heart of the Elements minions no longer have their base damage scale throughout progression. Instead, they are now boosted by minion damage bonuses. All stat boosts have been removed. <!-- This is due to the recipe change, see below -->
**[[Elemental in a Bottle]] and [[Rare Elemental in a Bottle]] minions immunity frames no longer scale with progression and now always grant enemies 20 local immunity frames. Elemental in a Bottle tornado spawner now always grants enemies 30 local immunity frames. Sand-Tornado now grants enemies 20 local immunity frames.
**[[Eye of the Storm]] minion immunity frames timers no longer scale with progression and now always grant enemies 20 local immunity frames. <!-- Storm mark immunity frames are always 30 local. -->
**[[Rose Stone]] minion immunity frames durations no longer scale with progression and now always grants enemies 20 local immunity frames. Increased base damage from 45 to 60. No longer increases any stats.
*Nerfed Forbidden Circlet set bonus projectile base damage from 75 to 40.
*Frost Flare no longer grants bonus life regeneration.
*Rebalanced [[Gladiator's Locket]], sword spirit minion immunity frames no longer scale with progression and now always grant 10 local immunity frames. Sword spirit base damage and rotation speed no longer scale with progression. Increased base damage from 20 to 30.
*Nerfed [[Hallowed Rune]]'s on-hit buff durations from 2 seconds to 1. Hallowed Defense modified from 7 defense and 7% damage reduction to 8 defense and 4% damage reduction. Decreased the damage granted of the Hallowed Power buff from 15% multiplicatively to 10% additively. Stars are now guaranteed to spawn instead of at a 33.33% chance, but cooldown has been increased from 1 second to 3.
*[[Hide of Astrum Deus]] now rains astral star projectiles on true melee hits. This effect has a 0.17-second internal cooldown.
*[[Hydrothermic armor]] stats adjusted:
**Hydrothermic armor base set bonus contact explosion now has a 100% chance to occur when hit instead of a 20% chance.
**[[Hydrothermic Mask]] 15% mana cost reduction has been moved from the headpiece to the set bonus.
**Hydrothermic Helmet set bonus flares now spawn once every 0.33 seconds instead of having a 50% chance to be spawned each time the player attacks.
*[[Jelly-Charged Battery]], [[Nucleogenesis]], [[Star-Tainted Generator]], [[Statis' Blessing]], and [[Statis' Curse]] all no longer stack minion slots bonuses with their downgrades.
*Jelly-Charged Battery, Hallowed Rune, [[Nuclear Rod]], Nucleogenesis, Star-Tainted Generator, and [[Voltaic Jelly]] bonus projectiles now have base projectile values. <!-- volt jelly/jelly bat = 15, rune = 30, nuke rod = 20, star core = 40, genesis = 60 -->
*[[lul]]'s invulnerability effect no longer has a Town NPC check.
*Nerfed [[Leaf Wings]] defense from 10 to 5 and damage reduction bonus from 10% to 5%.
*Nerfed [[Magic Hat]]'s magic damage and critical strike chance bonuses from 7% to 5%.
*Nerfed [[MOAB]] jump speed from 16% to 10%.
*Nerfed [[Mutated Truffle]]'s damage from 2000 to 1200.
*Nanotech now spawns in 3 bonus projectiles instead of 6 when a stealth strike is performed. Stealth strike bonus projectiles now have a cooldown of 1 second. Increased Spy damage bonus from 12% to 15%.
*Nebula armor orange Life Boosters no longer override negative life regeneration from debuffs.
*Nerfed Nebulous Core damage boost from 12% to 10%, and Nebulous Star projectile damage from 1500 to 250. Revival effect is now guaranteed, however, it now has a 90 second cooldown.
*Nerfed [[Normality Relocator]], bonus player fall speed after teleporting has been capped at 30 frames. While any boss is alive or any event is active, [[Chaos State]] is now inflicted for 15 seconds instead of 10 when used.
*Nerfed [[Old Die]], damage deviation range from {{color|+33%|green}} damage / {{color|-25%|red}} damage to {{color|+12.05%|green}} damage / {{color|-7.85%|red}} damage.
*Nerfed [[Omega Blue armor]] tentacles damage from 666 to 500, and set bonus armor penetration bonus from 50 to 15.
*[[Ornate Shield]] now allows the player to perform a dash that freezes enemies when double-tapping either the {{key|left}} or {{key|right}} key. When a shield bash is performed, it grants the player 4 immunity frames.
*[[Plague Hive]] no longer increases the damage of friendly bees, reduces the damage taken by the Plague debuff by 50%, and summons a damaging plague aura.
*Nerfed [[Plague Reaper armor]] set bonus ranged damage modifier from 100% to 60%, and critical strike damage modifier from 30% to 20%.
*[[Plaguebringer armor]] set bonus dash now grants the player 4 immunity frames when performing a shield bash.
*Nerfed [[Princess Spirit in a Bottle]] top mount speed from 107 mph to 86 mph.
*Nerfed [[Prismatic armor]] set bonus laser base damage from 50 to 30.
*Buffed [[Prismatic Helmet]] magic damage bonus from 8% to 18%.
*Prismatic Regalia now has a static 20% chance to fire homing rockets instead of a random chance between 1% and 80%.
*Nerfed [[Profaned Soul Artifact]] minion's base damage from 100 to 75, post-Devourer of Gods damage from 200 to 150, post-Yharon damage from 300 to 225. The offense guardian minion now deals 70% of the minion's damage.
*Nerfed [[Profaned Soul Crystal]]:
**Nerfed guardian minion's base damage from 800 to 495, 900 to 570 after defeating The Devourer of Gods, 1000 to 645 after defeating Yharon.
**Nerfed melee barrage spear's base damage from 1750 to 350.
**Nerfed melee main spear's base damage from 1250 to 250.
**Nerfed ranged fireball's base damage from 1000 to 200.
**Nerfed ranged meteor's base damage from 1500 to 300.
**Nerfed magic holy blast's base damage from 4500 to 900.
**Nerfed Spy ring shard's base damage from 880 to 176.
**Nerfed Spy main shard's base damage from 1100 to 220.
**The effects now also require the The Fabricational Quartet to be defeated.
*Increased [[Radiant Ooze]] life regeneration bonus while equipped at night from 1 to 4. Radiant Ooze life regeneration no longer stacks with the Ambrosial Ampoule.
*[[Rare Elemental in a Bottle]] healing orbs no longer home towards the player by default.
*Nerfed [[Reaper Tooth Necklace]] armor penetration bonus from 100 to 15 and damage bonus from 12% to 10%.
*Nerfed [[Regenator]] bonus life regeneration from 16 to 12. <!-- Technical: Also decreased lifeRegenTime from 8 to 6 -->
*Scuttler's Jewel projectiles no longer have a base damage and instead deal 35% of the weapon's damage.

*Buffed Sigil of Calamitas' magic damage bonus from 10% to 15%.

*Buffed [[Silva Wings]] flight time from 220 to 270.
*Nerfed [[Snow Ruffian armor]] set bonus fall velocity from 1.3x to 1.1x.
*[[Solar Flare armor]]'s dash now grants the player 12 immunity frames when a shield bash is performed. Buffed bash explosion damage from 200 to 400 and it now scales with melee damage bonuses.
*Buffed [[Spectral Veil]]'s teleport range from 20 tiles to 52.8125. Increased immunity time when dodging from 2 seconds to 2.5. Now passively increases movement speed by 10% and mobile stealth regeneration by 20%.

*Nerfed [[Spectre Wings]], removed 20 mana and 5% decreased mana cost bonuses from the Spectre Wings + [[Spectre Mask]] set bonus.
*Nerfed [[Spirit Glyph]]'s on-hit buff durations from 2 seconds to 1. Spirit Defense modified from 5 defense and 5% damage reduction to 6 defense and 3% damage reduction. Its damage buff from Spirit Power is now additive instead of multiplicative.
*The Sponge no longer inherits any effects of the [[Ambrosial Ampoule]] and its predecessors. Buffed damage reduction boost from 10% to 15%, defense boost from 10 to 20, health and mana boosts from 20 to 30, and healing on hit from 5% to 6.25%.
*[[Statis' Blessing]] and its upgrades now cause minions to inflict the Holy Flames debuff for 5 seconds instead of the Temporal Sadness debuff for 1 second.
*Buffed [[Statis' Ninja Belt]] jump speed bonus from 8% to 12%.
*Increased [[Statis' Void Sash]] dash length from 34 blocks to 38 and now grants full immunity for fall damage instead of increasing fall damage resistance. Nerfed Cosmic Scythe projectile damage from 500 to 375.
*[[Stardust Breastplate]] and [[Stardust Leggings]] now give 1 minion slot instead of 2.
*[[Sulphurous armor]] stats adjusted:
**Nerfed Sulphurous Helmet Spy damage bonus from 8% to 4%.
**Nerfed Sulphurous Breastplate Spy damage bonus from 10% to 8% and defense from 7 to 6.
**Nerfed Sulphurous Leggings defense from 6 to 5.
**Nerfed set bonus maximum stealth from 100 to 95.

*Nerfed [[Tarragon Horned Helm]] life aura damage from 150 to 120.
*Buffed [[Tarragon Mask]] magic damage bonus from 10% to 20%.
*[[Tarragon Visage]] set bonus no longer creates an explosion upon a ranged critical hit. Bonus leaf projectiles now always spawn instead of having a 25%-33% chance to spawn; this effect now has a 1 second cooldown. Decreased how many leaves can be spawned at once from 5 to 2. This change is inherited by the [[Auric Tesla Hooded Facemask]] set bonus.
*Buffed [[Tarragon Wings]] flight time from 210 to 250.

**Nerfed Monk's Bushy Brow Bald Cap melee speed bonus from 20% to 10%.

**Nerfed set bonus Spy damage from 20% to 15%.
**Set bonus now grants the player full immunity to knockback.
**Nerfed set bonus stealth strike explosion damage from 85 to 50, no longer inherits additional damage bonuses from the initial projectile but can still be affected by Spy damage bonuses.
**Decreased set bonus stealth strike explosion size and duration by 50%.
*Buffed [[Toxic Heart]] aura damage from 30 to 50. It now additionally reduces the damage and removes the blindness of the Plague debuff.
*[[Unstable Prism]] spark projectiles now have a base damage of 15 and no longer have a soft damage cap. Spark projectiles now linger for 1.5 seconds instead of 2.

*Nerfed [[Wizard Hat]] magic damage bonus from 15% to 5%.
*Buffed [[Wulfrum armor]] damage bonus for all sets from 3% to 10%.
*Nerfed [[Yharim's Gift]] exploding dust damage from 350 to 175.

==== Weapons ====
*All weapons that decrease an enemy's defense stat on hit now have their effects properly applied on enemies that set their defense every frame.
*All weapons with a melee and Spy variant are now either one damage type or the other:
**The following weapons now exclusively deal melee damage:
***[[Empyrean Knives]], [[Fallen Paladin's Hammer]], [[Galaxy Smasher]], [[Illustrious Knives]], [[Pwnagehammer]], [[Scourge of the Cosmos]], [[Stellar Contempt]], and [[Triactis' True Paladinian Mage-Hammer of Might]].
**The following weapons now exclusively deal Spy damage:
***[[Corpus Avertor]], [[Elemental Disk]], [[Eradicator]], [[Kelvin Catalyst]], [[Mangrove Chakram]], [[Nanoblack Reaper]], [[Seashell Boomerang]], [[Subduction Slicer]], and [[Terra Disk]].
*The damage of beam-type weapons and explosions that can infinitely pierce now have diminishing returns whenever an enemy is pierced.
*All projectiles that can home-in onto enemies have had their homing capabilities significantly decreased, in exchange, homing projectiles now home into enemies faster.
**Additionally split homing projectiles now no longer deal damage for the first 6 frames, in exchange, their velocities are multiplied even further when homing in onto an enemy.
*All friendly bee projectiles now inflict the Poisoned debuff for 2 seconds on hit.
*All friendly wasp projectiles now inflict the Poisoned and Venom debuffs for 2 seconds on hit.

Its stealth strike split projectiles now deal 60% of the weapon's damage instead of 75% of the weapon's damage. Stealth strikes now have a damage modifier of 122%.
*Nerfed [[Nychthemeron]], its stealth strikes now have a 67.5% damage modifier.
<!-- ROG:O --><!-- ROG:P -->
*Buffed [[Palladium Javelin]]'s base damage from 50 to 68. Its stealth strikes now have a 142.5% damage modifier.
*Nerfed [[Penumbra]]'s base damage from 1600 to 1008.
*Buffed [[Phantom Lance]], its stealth strikes now have a 175% damage modifier.
*Buffed [[Plaguenade]]'s base damage from 60 to 63.
*Nerfed [[Plasma Grenade]]'s base damage from 17,800 to 1378. Its stealth strikes now have a 105% damage modifier.
*Buffed [[Prismalline]]'s base damage from 18 to 24. Its stealth strikes now have a 115% damage modifier.
*Rebalanced [[Profaned Partisan]], buffed base damage from 231 to 280, its projectile now pierces 9 times instead of 5, but it grants enemies 12 local immunity frames instead of 10.
<!-- ROG:Q --><!-- ROG:R -->
*Rebalanced [[The Reaper]], buffed base damage from 150 to 153. Now has infinite enemy piercing. Stealth strikes now home in onto enemies more aggressively. However, stealth strike projectiles now grant enemies 32 local immunity frames instead of 15.
*Buffed [[Regulus Riot]], its stealth strikes now have a 120% damage modifier.
<!-- ROG:S -->
*Nerfed [[Scarlet Devil]]'s base damage from 40,000 to 8000. Its explosions now grant enemies 10 local immunity frames instead of 1. Stealth strike spear now additionally causes a large pattern of homing bullets to erupt on hit.
*Rebalanced [[Sealed Singularity]], buffed base damage from 169 to 260 and its projectile velocity has been increased from 12 to 14. However, its stealth strikes now deal 80% of the weapon's damage.
*Rebalanced [[Shard of Antumbra]], increased its use time from 16 to 24. However, its stealth strikes now have a 150% damage modifier.
*Rebalanced Shattered Sun, nerfed base damage from 82 to 75. However, its damage no longer scales with distance. Explosions now deal 55% of the weapon's damage instead of 70%.
*Buffed [[Shock Grenade]]'s initial velocity from 7.5 to 12.5.
*Nerfed [[Skyfin Bombers]], stealth strike now only deals 75% of the weapon's damage, stealth strike projectiles now travel and turn much slower.
*Nerfed [[Star of Destruction]], its stealth strikes now have an 80% damage modifier.
*Rebalanced [[Stellar Knife]], its stealth strike now throws out 10 knives instead of 20, however, the knife limit has been increased from 10 to 20. If there are more than 20 in existence only 1 knife will be thrown regardless if the attack is a stealth strike. Stealth strikes now have a 110% damage modifier.
*Buffed Subduction Slicer, its stealth strikes now have a 150% damage modifier.
*Rebalanced [[Supernova]], nerfed base damage from 2250 to 675. Increased its explosion size by 50%. However, it no longer inflicts the Glacial State debuff. Increased stealth strike damage by 8% but decreased energy trail damage from 80% to 48%.
*Nerfed [[Sylvan Slasher]]'s base damage from 87 to 52. Increased stealth generation per hit from 1% to 10%.
*Buffed [[The Syringe]], its stealth strikes now have a 120% damage modifier.
*Nerfed [[System Bane]], its lingering projectile damage values now have diminishing returns depending on how many bane projectiles currently exist.
<!-- ROG:T -->
*Rebalanced [[Tarragon Throwing Dart]]'s base damage from 380 to 330, and its projectile velocity has been decreased from 24 to 12. However, its projectile now pierces enemies 6 times instead of 3, and now grants enemies 10 ID-static immunity frames instead of 3 local immunity frames.
*Nerfed [[Terra Disk]], its stealth strikes now have a 90% damage modifier.
*Buffed [[Terror Talons]], its stealth strikes now have a 487.5% damage modifier instead of 325%.
*Nerfed Time Bolt's base damage from 720 to 432. It now inflicts the Time Distortion debuff for 1 second instead of 3 and its stealth strikes now inflict it for 2 seconds instead of 6. Stealth strikes now deal 68% of the weapon's damage.
*Buffed [[Totality Breakers]], its stealth strikes now have a 115% damage modifier.
*Nerfed [[Toxicant Twister]]'s base damage from 488 to 323.
<!-- ROG:U -->
*Nerfed [[Utensil Poker]]'s base damage from 540 to 480 and Butcher Knife projectile damage modifier decreased from +40% to -40%. Additionally, its main attack has been fixed so that each attack in the burst counts as a separate attack, and the tooltip has been updated to reflect this change.
<!-- ROG:V -->
*Nerfed [[Valediction]]'s base damage from 405 to 243 and its stealth strike typhoon projectiles can now only be spawned once. Decreased stealth strike typhoon projectile damage from 50% of the weapon's damage to 30%.
<!-- ROG:W -->
*Nerfed Wave Pounder's base damage from 102 to 75.

===== Multi-Class and Classless =====
*Nerfed [[Aether's Whisper]]'s base damage from 1150 to 504.
*Nerfed [[Prismatic Breaker]]'s base damage from 1200 to 662. Its use time is now static at 13. It no longer inflicts the Glacial State debuff. <!-- Technically use time equalizing is a bug fix for left click -->

==== Miscellaneous ====
*Nerfed [[Archery Potion]]'s arrow and bow damage modifier from 1.2x to 1.05x.
*Nerfed [[Arctic Arrow]]s, they now inflict the Glacial State debuff for 1 second instead of 2.
*The [[Astral Infection (debuff)|Astral Infection]], [[God Slayer Inferno]], [[Lethal Lava Burn]] and [[Venom]] debuffs now decrease player movement speed by 15% when inflicted.
*Caribbean Rum and Cinnamon Roll buffs now decrease defense by 10% instead of by 12 defense.
*Decreased Cinnamon Roll damage modifier from 150% to 110%. <!-- Tooltip changed to match -->
*Rebalanced [[Chlorophyte Bullet]]s, buffed base damage from 7 to 10. Decreased homing strength by 33% and dust created by 50%.
*Nerfed Crush Depth's effectiveness on enemies from 400 to 180 reduced life regeneration in Hardmode.
*The Electrified debuff now deals 50% less damage while any boss is alive.
*The Elysian meteor projectiles summoned by [[Elysian Arrow]]s now deal 50% of the weapon's damage.
*Everclear buff now decreases defense by 30% instead of by 40 defense.
*Decreased Evergreen Gin damage modifier from 150% to 110%. <!-- Tooltip changed to match -->
*Nerfed [[Explosive Shotgun Shell]]'s base damage from 24.48% of an enemy's total HP to 6.4%.
*Nerfed [[Extreme Gravity]] flight time penalty from 50% to 25%.
*Fabsol's Vodka buff now decreases defense by 10% instead of by 20 defense.
*Decreased Fireball damage modifier from 110% to 105%. <!-- Tooltip changed to match -->
*Grape Beer buff now decreases defense by 3% instead of by 2 defense.
*Nerfed [[Grenade Shells]]'s base damage from 11.4% of an enemy's total HP to 3.4%.
*The [[Holy Inferno]] debuff now decreased player movement speed by 25% when inflicted.
*Nerfed [[Holy Fire Bullet]]'s base damage from 27 to 22.
*Nerfed [[Icarus' Folly]] flight time penalty from 75% to 33%.
*Nerfed [[Icicle Arrow]]s, decreased shard projectile damage from 50% of the weapon's damage to 33.33%.
*Nerfed [[Icy Bullet]]s, they now inflict the Glacial State debuff for 1 second instead of 2.
*The [[Irradiated]], [[Poisoned]], [[Warped]], and [[Whispering Death]] debuffs now decrease player movement speed by 10% when inflicted.
*[[Lore items]] no longer grant additional bonuses when favorited.
*Nerfed [[Magic Power Potion]]'s magic damage modifier from 20% to 10%.
*Nerfed [[Magnum Rounds]], removed critical hit damage modifier and decreased base damage from 4.48% of an enemy's total HP to 1.813%.
*Margarita, Star Beam Rye, and White Wine buffs now decrease defense by 6% instead of by 6 defense.
*Decreased the [[Mechanical Cart]]'s top movement speed by 30%.
*Nerfed [[Portal Gun]], maximum player fall speed after teleporting decreased from 3x to 2x.
*Buffed [[Relic of Convergence]], increased HP granted to the player after a successful cast from 35 to 70.
*Nerfed [[Rocket III]] base damage from 65 to 49.
*Nerfed [[Rocket IV]] base damage from 65 to 49.
*Rum buff now decreases defense by 5% instead of by 8 defense.
*Decreased Screwdriver modifier from 110% to 105%. <!-- Tooltip changed to match -->
*[[Spiteful Candle]] buff no longer ignores timed DR for boss fights.
*Nerfed [[Sugar Rush]] buff's movement speed bonus from 20% to 10%.
*Nerfed [[Swiftness Potion]] buff's movement speed bonus from 25% to 15%.
*Decreased Tequila Sunrise buff defense bonus during daytime from 15 to 10.
*Buffed [[Terra Arrow]]'s base damage from 9 to 14.
*Nerfed [[Tesla Potion]], its aura now inflicts enemies with the Galvanic Corrosion debuff for 2 seconds instead of 3.
*Nerfed [[Vanquisher Arrow]] base damage from 33 to 22. Increased the cooldown for arrow splits by 50%.
*Vodka buff now decreases defense by 5% instead of by 4 defense.
*Whiskey buff now decreases defense by 5% instead of by 8 defense.
*Yharim's Stimulants buff now increases pick speed by 5%.

*[[Arcanum of the Void]] and [[Spectral Veil]] are now both Expert Mode exclusive drops, now have the {{rare|Rainbow}} rarity, and drop from their corresponding bosses' treasure bag. <!-- In the bags, twisting nether and dark plasma yield is 3-4 and armored shells are 7-10. -->
*[[Abyssal Crate]], [[Aquatic Scourge]] and [[Cnidrion]] no longer drop [[Coral]], [[Seashell]]s, [[Starfish]], and [[Victory Shard]]s. [[Eye of Cthulhu]] no longer drops Victory Shards.

*[[Bat Hook]] now drops from [[Vampire]]s at a {{dv|2.5%|5%}} chance. They no longer appear from the Goodie Bag.
*Added block-drop code that prevents instances of the same item being dropped twice by an enemy.
*Increased [[Bloom Stone]], [[Chaos Stone]], [[Cryo Stone]], and [[Rose Stone]] drop rates from 10% to 20%.
*Bloom Stone now drops from Plantera instead of The Plaguebringer Goliath.
*Cryogen no longer drops [[Frost Core]]s.
*Explosive Shotgun Shells, Grenade Shells, and Magnum Rounds can no longer drop from any boss. They are now sold exclusively by the Arms Dealer.

**Grenade Shells will be sold for {{gc|5}} if the Bazooka is in the player's inventory.
**Magnum Rounds will be sold for {{gc|3}} if either the Magnum, Lightning Hawk, or Elephant Killer are in the player's inventory.

*The Slime God's Treasure Bag no longer contains extra Gel. Its Purified Gel drop yield is additionally changed from 35-55 to 40-52.
*The [[Starter Bag]] now drops a [[Tin Broadsword]], [[Tin Bow]], [[Tin Hammer]], and [[Topaz Staff]] instead of their Copper variants if the world generates with Tin ore.
*The Starter Bag will no longer drop a Slime Crown.
*Skeletron will now drop between 75 and 100 [[Bone]]s.
*[[Sulphuric Scale]]s no longer have redacted drop rates after defeating the Aquatic Scourge.
*Supreme Calamitas can no longer drop [[lul]]. <!-- It also has a tooltip now… unless…? - Merkalto -->

=== Recipes ===
*The availability of post-Devourer of Gods items has been restructured:
**The following recipes now require an [[Ancient Manipulator]] to be crafted and instead of Draedon's Forge:
***[[Ascendant Spirit Essence]], Effulgent Manipulator, Otherworldly furniture set
**The following recipes now require a Cosmic Anvil to be crafted instead of Draedon's Forge:
***Ace's High, Aetherflux Cannon, Alluvion, Alpha Ray, The Amalgam, Anti-materiel Rifle, Ark of the Cosmos, Ataraxia, Auric Bar, all Auric Tesla armor pieces, Auric Toilet, Celestial Tracers, Cleansing Blaze, Core of the Blood God, Corvid Harbinger Staff, Cosmic Shiv, Cosmic Viper Engine, Cosmilite Brick, Cosmilite furniture set, Dark Sun Ring, Devil's Devastation, The Devourer of Cods, Draedon's Heart, Dragon Pow, Dragonfruit, Drataliornus, Eclipse Mirror, Eclipse's Fall,  Elemental Gauntlet, Elemental Quiver, Elysian Tracers, Empyrean Knives, Endo Hydra Staff, The Enforcer, Essence Flayer, Ethereal Talisman, Executioner's Blade, Eye of Extinction, Face Melter, all Fearmonger armor pieces, Galaxia, Galaxy Smasher, Gaze of Crysthamyr, God's Paranoia, all God Slayer armor pieces, Greatsword of Blah, Hypothermia, Karasawa, Light God's Brilliance, Midnight Sun Beacon, Minigun, Nadir, Nanotech, Nucleogenesis, Omega Healing Potion, Onyxia, The Oracle, The Pack, Penumbra, Phangasm, Phaseslayer, Plasma Grenade, Pole Warper, Prismatic Breaker, Pulse Rifle, Rampart of Deities, Rubico Prime, Saros Possession, Scourge of the Cosmos, SDFMG, all Silva armor pieces, Silva Crystals, Silva Wings, The Sponge, Starmada, Statis' Void Sash, Stream Gouge, Soul Piercer, Tesla Cannon, Tyranny's End, Ultima, Vanquisher Arrow, Voltaic Climax, and The Wand. <!-- Shadowspec/Exo stuff still requires Draedon's Forge -->
**Ascendant Spirit Essence now requires an additional 2 [[Darksun Fragment]]s to craft.
**[[Ice Barrage]] is now crafted at an Ice Machine instead of Draedon's Forge.
*All recipes that previously used both Ascendant Spirit Essence and Darksun Fragments as crafting materials now just require Ascendant Spirit Essence with a 5:1 ratio. The following were additionally adjusted:
***[[Apotheosis]] now requires 11 Ascendant Spirit Essences to be crafted instead of 7.
**Cleansing Blaze, Cosmic Viper Engine, Essence Flayer, Executioner's Blade, Galaxy Smasher, Soul Piercer, Stream Gouge, The Devourer of Cods, The Enforcer, and Vanquisher Arrows no longer use Nightmare Fuel or Endothermic Energy in their recipes.
**Cosmic Shiv no longer requires Darksun Fragments and Ascendant Spirit Essences to be crafted.
*Standardized consumable Spy weapon recipe yields:
**Tarragon Throwing Dart recipe now yields 333 items. <!-- Post ML consumables give 333 except Plasma nade, which gives 999 -->
**[[Adamantite Throwing Axe]], [[Cobalt Kunai]], [[Crystal Piercer]], Fantasy Talisman, [[Feather Knife]], [[Infernal Kris]], [[Hardened Honeycomb]], [[Iron Francisca]], [[Lead Tomahawk]], [[Orichalcum Spiked Gemstone]], Plaguenade, [[Palladium Javelin]], [[Rot Ball]], Shard of Antumbra, Shock Grenade, [[Sludge Splotch]], [[Titanium Shuriken]], [[Tooth Ball]], and [[Wulfrum Knife]] recipes now yield 100 items. <!-- Most pre-hm and hm consumables give 100 -->
**[[Bouncy Spiky Ball]], [[Sticky Spiky Ball]], [[Throwing Brick]], and [[Web Ball]] recipes now yield 20 items. <!-- Super easy to get consumables yield 20 -->
**Bouncy Spiky Ball and Sticky Spiky Ball recipes now require 20 Spiky Balls to be crafted instead of 3.
**Fantasy Talisman now requires 1 Silk and 1 Ectoplasm to be crafted instead of 3 Silk and 5 Ectoplasm.
**Plaguenades now require 20 Beenades and 5 Plague Cell Canisters to be crafted instead of 15 Beenades and 3 Plague Cell Canisters.
**Shock Grenades now require 20 Grenades, 5 Martian Conduit Plating, and 5 Nanites to be crafted instead of 10 Grenades, 2 Martian Conduit Plating, and 1 Nanite.
**Sludge Splotch now requires 1 Purified Gel and 1 Tissue Sample or Shadow Scale instead of 2 Purified Gel and 5 Tissue Samples or Shadow Scales.
