=== Reworks ===
*Reworked [[Aetherflux Cannon]], it now fires two yellow lasers that travel in a double helix pattern, capable of piercing one target and traveling through solid tiles. Firing delay changed from 0.4-0.1 seconds over a 1 second wind-up to 0.08 seconds, and can now benefit from attack speed boosts. Nerfed base damage from 300 to 247, buffed mana cost from 10 to 8, and nerfed its use time from 12 to 36. The gun itself no longer deals contact damage.
*Reworked [[Apoctosis Array]] and [[Ion Blaster]], damage scaling is now based on how full the player's maximum mana is instead of how high the player's mana is. Maximum projectile damage is 160%, minimum projectile damage is 20%. Using the [[Astral Injection]] will cap its maximum damage at 111%. Buffed Apoctosis Array base damage from 55 to 99. Buffed Ion Blaster base damage from 30 to 43.
*Reworked Apotheosis, it no longer fires a spectral worm head and instead spawns a cosmic portal that releases a mini Devourer of Gods that will swarm an enemy and fire lasers. Nerfed base damage from 420 to 77, its use time from 15 to 50, and its mana cost from 42 to 300.
*Reworked [[Baleful Harvester]], no longer fires flaming skull projectiles when used. It now summons a swarm of flaming pumpkins and skulls that explode into homing flares on enemy hits. Flaming Pumpkins deal 50% less damage if they critically hit. Baleful Harvester now does 50% more damage when hitting an enemy with the weapon itself. Nerfed base damage from 160 to 90 and gave it a 150% size modifier.
*Reworked [[Blood Boiler]], no longer required to be used in 10 second bursts; it now has a use time of 5. Its projectile size has been increased by 50% and its range has been increased by 20%. Now has a 25% chance to not consume the player's health as ammo when used. Nerfed base damage from 400 to 250, but its projectile now grants enemies 5 local immunity frames instead of 10.
*Reworked Boss Effects, the debuff now lists all effects that are currently in effect during the boss encounter. Boss Effects will now be removed from the player if they travel more than 400 blocks away from the boss.
**Boss Effects now disables all teleportation effects and decreases the Portal Gun falling speed modifier from 3.5x to 2x in addition to all of the previous effects of Boss Zen.
*Reworked [[The Burning Sky]], now functions similarly to a staff rather than a broadsword. Fireballs are casted in a single wave instead of three, each with 6 fireballs instead of 10. The spawning range decreased horizontally and increased vertically, and velocity spread greatly decreased. Buffed base damage from 200 to 244, and its use time from 14 to 9. Decreased velocity from 35 to 14.
*Reworked [[Chaos Stone]], all previous stats removed, it now replaces Mana Sickness with the Mana Burn debuff when consuming Mana Potions. Mana Burn drains the player's health instead of reducing magic damage.
*Reworked [[Chicken Cannon]], now grants enemies 15 local immunity frames instead of 1. Buffed base damage from 12 to 416, but increased its use time from 13 to 33, decreased velocity from 24 to 14.5, and terminal rocket velocity decreased by 15%. Added a right-click function to instantly detonate existing rockets.
*Reworked [[Conference Call]], now fires 5 bullets per use instead of 6. Up to 12 additional enemies can now be targeted and have bullets fired at them. Extra bullets now deal 70% of the weapon's damage. Buffed base damage from 35 to 40.
*Reworked [[Cryo Stone]], all previous stats removed, it now spawns an ice shield around the player that will damage and slow enemies on contact.
*Reworked Cryophobia, now fires a single ball of ice that splits into three waves of ice that grow over time. Increased its use time from 22 to 28. Projectile piercing decreased from 4 enemies to 3. It now inflicts the Glacial State debuff for 1 second instead of 2.
*Reworked [[Dazzling Stabber Staff]], removed bonus homing knife projectile, now summons a dazzling knife that idles above the player's head that targets nearby enemies and travels in an ellipse around the enemy in-between hits. Dazzling knives now release a glowing trail behind them as they move. Buffed base damage from 40 to 53 but increased its use time from 12 to 14.
*Reworked Defiled Feather, no longer boosts item drop rates, doubles coin drop rates, and allows enemies to critically hit the player. Now toggled by favoriting and unfavoriting the item.
*Reworked [[Deep Sea Dumbbell]], flex damage no longer boosts stealth strike damage and instead boosts the damage of the next dumbbell throw by 100%, can stack up to 4 times. Hitting an enemy with a dumbbell no longer permanently decreases enemy defense by 15. Nerfed base damage from 900 to 466. Stealth strikes now have a 130% damage modifier. Increased critical strike chance from 2% to 4%.
*Reworked both the "Dodge" and "Projectile Reflect" mechanics for coverings that have a chance for the player to avoid all damage. They now utilize a global cooldown in exchange for a guaranteed chance to dodge or reflect damage. If a dodge or reflect is performed, no other dodge or reflect can be performed until the global cooldown has ended.
**After performing a reflect from the [[Arcanum of the Void]], [[Astral Arcanum]], [[Daedalus Helm]] set bonus, [[The Evolution]], or [[The Transformer]], the player will be temporarily immune and granted 20 immunity frames. <!-- Gael's Greatsword is unchanged and still provides 4 iframes with the same effects -->
**The [[Abyssal Mirror]], [[Black Belt]], [[Daedalus Helm]] set bonus, [[Eclipse Mirror]], [[Master Ninja Gear]], [[Statis' Ninja Belt]], and [[Statis' Void Sash]] will cause a 90 second global cooldown when the player dodges.
***The Daedalus Helm set bonus reflected projectiles will now deal 50% of their damage to the player.
**The [[Arcanum of the Void]] and [[Astral Arcanum]] will cause a 75 second global cooldown when the player reflects a projectile.
**[[The Evolution]] will cause a 120 second global cooldown when it reflects a projectile.
**Armageddon now has an alternative toggle that disables all dodge effects.
**All reflected projectiles no longer have a 200% damage modifier.
**Projectiles that deal 0 damage can no longer be reflected. <!-- also includes: Brimstone Monsters, Exo Nukes, Beams/Deathrays, and Birb Aura -->
*Reworked [[Dodu's Handcannon]], no longer fires five high-velocity bullets and instead fires an explosive nut that damages nearby enemies when it explodes. Buffed base damage from 485 to 857 but removed its bonus +26% critical strike chance.
*Reworked [[Dragon's Breath]], now fires a burst of regular bullets along with another burst of special bullets followed by a short delay. Added a secondary attack to remove the delay and randomize the type of bullets fired at the cost of 20% damage. Nerfed base damage from 260 to 198 but buffed its use time from 20 to 18.
*Reworked Dragon Rage, when used the player now swings a double-bladed baton that causes a small explosion whenever an enemy is hit. After 10 enemy hits, 10 to 15 homing fireballs are released that deal 12.5% of the weapon's damage. Buffed base damage from 800 to 1275.
*Reworked [[The Enforcer]], no longer fires out a flame burst when swung and now randomly spawns five lingering essence flames on enemy hit. Nerfed base damage from 1900 to 890.
*Reworked Ethereal Extorter, no longer grants additional bonuses based on the Moon Phase and biome the player is in. Now causes all Spy projectiles to explode into 5-12 lost souls and grants +10 Spy stealth. While equipped it will still grant a 10% damage bonus in exchange for -1 life regeneration.
*Reworked [[Evil Smasher]], no longer spawns rock spikes on enemy hits or has a random chance for its stats to double. It now gains 10% increased damage, attack speed and knockback per enemy defeated with the Evil Smasher up to 10 defeats. If the player uses another item, all stacks are removed. If the player is hit by an enemy, one stack will be removed. Buffed base damage from 55 to 125. Nerfed base knockback from 8 to 6, and its use time from 29 to 37.
*Reworked [[Flame-Licked Shell]], now decreases defense granted by 50% after being hit, during that time the player is granted full movement speed. It also now grants the player heat protection in Death Mode and 7 seconds of lava immunity. Increased defense granted from 35 to 36.
*Reworked [[Gamma Heart]], Gamma Hydra heads now move sporadically when summoned and fire a Gamma Canister into the air towards enemies that explode into 10 Gamma Bullets. Gamma Bullets will spread out and subsequently home back in on enemies one second later. Gamma Bullets can no longer travel through tiles. Gamma Hydras can no longer directly fire Gamma Bullets or Gamma Deathrays. Buffed base damage from 120 to 187 and all projectiles now inflict the Irradiated debuff instead of Electrified. <!-- Canisters now fire 45% faster compared to before. -->
*Reworked [[Ghastly Visage]], ghastly blast projectiles no longer split into 5-8 explosions that split into 3-6 shards, and now split into 5 shards and 5 explosions with each explosion further splitting into 3 shard projectiles. All projectiles now grant enemies 8 local immunity frames instead of 4, and home in onto enemies 20% faster. Nerfed base damage from 92 to 80.
*Reworked [[Ghoulish Gouger]], no longer throws out one ghoulish scythe that grants enemies 2 local immunity frames; stealth strikes no longer spawn waves of souls. When used it now consecutively throws out four ghoulish scythes at a very high velocity that grant enemies 10 local immunity frames. Stealth strike now spawns in three tormented souls on the first enemy hit that deals 85% of the weapon's damage. Buffed base damage from 110 to 166.
*The [[Goblin Tinkerer]]'s reforge system has been reworked:
**All modifiers are grouped into categories that consist of 7 tiers (0-6), categories are determined by weapon damage type and ability to receive certain modifiers over others.
**Each time the player reforges an item, the tier gets higher until tier 6 is reached. Any reforges past tier 6 will reset the tier to 0.
***Rolling an item to tier 0 will grant a random modifier that weapon can receive.
**Modifiers get progressively more powerful the higher the tier. Rolling an item to tier 6 will always grant the best modifier possible for its category (For example, if the [[Crystalline]] is currently at tier 5, its next reforge would grant it the '''Flawless''' modifier).
**Weapons can no longer receive negative modifiers when reforging.
**If an covering has the highest tier modifier it can obtain, only the highest tier modifier can be rolled if that item is reforged (For example, if the [[Coin of Deceit]] currently has the '''Silent''' modifier, its next reforge has the potential to roll the '''Lucky''' modifier, but can not roll the '''Spiked''' modifier).
**All weapons can now receive their class type's best modifier except for Flails, Spears, and Yoyos. Weapons that have no knockback cannot gain knockback increasing modifier bonuses. Ranged weapons that have a use time lower than 6 will not receive any speed-adjusting modifier bonuses. Mage weapons that have a mana cost lower than 5 will not receive any mana cost decreasing modifier bonuses.
**All reforge value costs have been decreased by 50%. This includes all modifiers introduced by the Calamity Mod.

*Reworked [[God Slayer armor]] and [[Silva armor]]:
**God Slayer armor now only utilizes the [[God Slayer Horned Greathelm]], [[God Slayer Helmet]], and [[God Slayer Mask]] headpieces. The [[God Slayer Horned Helm]] and [[God Slayer Visage]] are no longer associated with the armor set and are now vanity headpieces that drop from the [[Sentinels of the Devourer]].
**The [[God Slayer Chestplate]] no longer grants the player a 2% chance to avoid all damage from an attack.
**The God Slayer armor base set bonus is no longer a revive that has a 45 second cooldown. The base set bonus now allows the player to dash for 8 seconds by holding {{key|God Slayer Dash}}. The player can be launched in 1 of 8 directions depending on which movement keys are currently being held down. Dashes have a base damage of 3000 and inflict the God Slayer Inferno debuff for 10 seconds. While dashing, the player is invulnerable and is cleansed of most debuffs. This dash has a 35 second cooldown and is inherited by the Auric Tesla Royal Helm, Auric Tesla Hooded Facemask, and Auric Tesla Plumed Helm set bonuses. <!-- if the player is hindered, is using a hook, or on a rope, the dash can not be performed. -->
***The dash cleanse will apply to the following debuffs:
{{collapse top|God Slayer dash debuff immunities}}
{{:Immunity item debuffs}}
{{collapse bottom}}
**[[God Slayer Cooldown]] no longer decreases the player's life regeneration by 50%. This change is inherited by the Auric Tesla Royal Helm, Auric Tesla Hooded Facemask, and Auric Tesla Plumed Helm set bonuses.
**The [[God Slayer Helmet]] set bonus shrapnel projectiles now always fire once every 2.5 seconds instead of having a 5% chance to be fired. Nerfed projectile base damage from 960 to 675.
**The God Slayer Helmet set bonus no longer grants additional damage modifiers to ranged weapon critical hits.
**Silva armor now only utilizes the [[Silva Masked Cap]] and the Silva Horned Hood. The [[Silva Helm]], [[Silva Horned Helm]], and [[Silva Mask]] are no longer associated with the armor set, and are now vanity headpieces that drop from The Devourer of Gods.
**Silva Immunity now cleanses most debuffs when applied and will grant the player immunity to all damage for 8 seconds. The debuffs cleansed are shared with the God Slayer armor dash.
**Silva armor no longer grants bonus damage modifiers after Silva Immunity ends.
**Silva armor healing orbs are now capped at 10 HP.
**Nerfed Silva Horned Hood crystal projectile base damage from 1500 to 600. Moved 10% of its total summon damage bonus to the headpiece itself.
**Silva Masked Cap set bonus explosions now have a 100% chance to proc once every 5 seconds instead of having a 10% chance for explosions to proc per use.
*Reworked [[Godly Soul Artifact]], it no longer summons two Sons of Yharon minions and instead increases the player's minion slots by 1 each time a Son of Yharon is summoned using Yharon's Kindle Staff.
*Reworked [[Hellborn]], contact explosions now increase the weapon's base damage, fire rate, and knockback by 600% for 10 seconds. The contact damage explosion effect decays over 10 seconds. Performing a contact explosion while the boost is decaying will reset the boost to 600%. Decreased contact damage modifier from 15x to 10x. Nerfed base damage from 18 to 14, and its use time from 9 to 10.
*Reworked Magnetic Meltdown, no longer spawns a spread of 3-7 magnetic orbs that fire 30 shadowbolts per second. When used, it now fires four magnetic orbs in a diamond pattern that travel for a short distance, firing 12 shadowbolts per second at nearby enemies. Nerfed base damage from 90 to 60.
*Reworked Midnight Sun Beacon, removed beacon laser beam barrage. Midnight Sun UFO minions now predict enemy movement and fire laser pellets for 50% longer. Pellet projectile velocities increased by 225%. UFO minions now lock onto targets 250% faster. While performing their laser barrage, UFO minions will now circle above their target. Laser beam size and length are now based on how large their target is. Nerfed base damage from 240 to 191. <!-- There's also iframe shenanigans -->
*Movement Speed has been reworked and will now grant proper bonuses to both player movement and flight speed, the static movement cap of any boots that grant the ability to run has been removed. Additionally, the maximum horizontal player speed cap has been increased from 160% to 300%.
**All [[Balloons|Balloon]] coverings now grant the player +15% jump speed instead of +30%.
**Equipping any type of wing covering will now grant the player +24% jump speed. This is added on to any other jump speed boosts the wing covering might have.
**The player is granted 10% jump speed if neither wings nor balloons are equipped.
**Flight time boosts are also now additive instead of multiplicative on top of receiving bonuses from movement speed.
**The '''Brisk''', '''Fleeting''', '''Hasty''', and '''Quick''' modifiers now increase movement speed by {{color|+2%|green}} / {{color|+4%|green}} / {{color|+6%|green}} / {{color|+8%|green}} respectively.

**[[Asphalt Block]]s will grant 75% increased movement speed while running atop them without [[Hermes Boots]] and its upgrades, however, will only increase player acceleration and traction by 75% if the player runs atop them with Hermes Boots and its upgrades.
**[[Ice Skates]] and its upgrades no longer increase player movement speed or acceleration while on [[Frozen Slime Block]]s and [[Ice Block]]s.
*Reworked [[Omniblade]], now uses a holdout projectile that slashes in front of the player. Buffed base damage from 63 to 100. <!-- Note, use time should now be listed as 10 since it no longer uses style 1. -->
*Reworked Pearl God, it now fires a shockblast that heals the player for 5 HP. Each time a shockblast is released, additional bullets are fired that have an even spread. The number of bullets increases by 1 each time the weapon is used, up to 6 times. Bullets deal 50% of the weapon's damage. Every seventh shot of this weapon will always fire a massive shockblast that explodes on contact with blocks and enemies. Buffed base damage from 40 to 125.
*Reworked [[Phantasmal Ruin]], projectile collision now spawns four phantasmal spirits that will home in onto enemies shortly after being spawned. Phantasmal spirit projectiles deal 10% of the weapon's damage instead of 5%. Lost soul projectiles are now spawned once every 0.5 seconds. Stealth strikes cause cloned projectiles to spawn every 0.25 seconds that deal 25% of the weapon's damage instead of 20% that weakly home in onto enemies. Cloned projectiles can no longer hit enemies twice. Stealth strikes now have a damage modifier of 122%.Nerfed base damage from 1500 to 995, and its use time from 25 to 35. <!-- This weapon is such a mess and is likely going to be rebalanced again. -Merkalto -->
*Reworked [[Rage Meter]]:
**Decreased Rage Mode damage from 50% to 35%. <!-- Compensation nerf, still a net-buff overall. -->
**The Rage Meter now has a maximum value of 100.
**The Rage Meter now builds when nearby enemies, the closer the enemy is to the player the faster the Rage Meter will build.
***The fastest rate the Rage Meter will build is 3.33 per second when within 6.25 blocks (100 pixels) of an enemy and the slowest rate the Rage Meter will build is 0.15 per second when 50 blocks (800 pixels) away from an enemy.
**The Rage meter will build 300% faster during boss fights. <!-- DOES NOT INCLUDE: Worm boss body and tail segments, boss servants, and somehow randomly spawned enemies if anything spawns with Boss Zen. -->
**If the player exits combat for 10 seconds, the Rage Meter will now diminish at a base rate of 5% per second.
**When activated, the Rage Mode buff will drain the Rage Meter at a base rate of 11.11% per second and will take 9 seconds to drain completely. This buff prevents the player from building additional rage.
**The [[Mushroom Plasma Root]], [[Infernal Blood]], and [[Red Lightning Container]] now increase the Rage buff's duration by 1 second each, causing the Rage Meter to drain at a rate of 10% per second with one upgrade, 9.09% per second with two upgrades, and 8.33% per second with all three upgrades.
**The [[Heart of Darkness]] will no longer increase the chances of the player receiving the [[Absolute Rage]] buff or give additional Rage buff damage and will now prevent the Rage Meter from naturally draining while the player is not in combat. <!-- Reminder: Draedon's Heart no longer inherit HoD due to being repurposed to The Fabricational Quartet -->
**Gael's Greatsword bonus skull attack will still consume the Rage Meter when activated.
*Reworked [[Reaver armor]]:
**All headpieces no longer grant class-specific bonuses. However, all headpieces now grant temporary immunity to Lava and still retain the ability to move freely through liquids.
***[[Reaver Headgear]] now grants 10 defense instead of 7, 10% increased pickaxe speed, and 20% increased tile placement speed. Removed 15% increased Spy damage, 5% increased Spy velocity and critical strike chance, and 20% increased movement speed bonuses.
***[[Reaver Helm]] now grants 30 defense instead of 25, 15% increased damage reduction, and +50 maximum HP. However, all damage the player does is decreased by 30%. Removed 15% increased melee damage, 10% increased melee speed, 5% increased melee critical strike chance, and 10% increased movement speed bonuses.
***[[Reaver Visage]] now grants 25% increased movement speed and 10% increased jump speed. It still grants 13 defense. Removed 5% increased ranged damage, 20% decreased ammo usage, 5% increased ranged critical strike chance, and 10% increased movement speed bonuses.
**Reaver Headgear set bonus now causes all nearby treasure to sparkle, increases item grab and tile placement range, causes all tiles mined while submerged in water to restore 10% of the player's breath meter, reduces enemy aggression towards the player, and summons a Reaver Orb that provides light. It no longer grants the player additional Spy class benefits including granting a maximum of 110 Spy stealth and releasing spores when hit.
**Reaver Helm set bonus now grants an additional 10 defense points and +3 life regeneration, decreases any life regeneration decreases from debuffs (this calculation is rounded to a whole number), decreases the player's movement speed and flight time by 20%, increases the player's aggro, causes all enemy damage to be reflected with a 5:1 ratio creating a thorn spike each time that effect occurs, and causes all damage dealt to have a 10% chance to spawn a reaver health orb and temporarily increase life regeneration by 1. The '''Reaver Rage''' buff can still be given to the player with a 25% chance when damaged, which now grants +30% increased damage, 5 defense, and 5% increased melee swing speed. It no longer grants the player additional melee class benefits, including the reaver explosions.
**Reaver Visage set bonus now grants immunity to fall damage and allows for auto jump. It also now increases flight time and horizontal wing speed by 10%, causes all hooks to deploy and retract 10% faster and reduces the cooldown of dashes by 33%. It no longer grants additional ranged class benefits and creates reaver rockets.
**There is no rework to the Reaver Helmet, Reaver Mask, and their corresponding set bonuses due to their removal.
*Reworked [[Skullmasher]], high-velocity bullets now spawn six additional bullets towards enemies that travel 50% slower and deal 10% of the weapon's damage. Nerfed base damage from 2310 to 924.
*Reworked Telluric Glare, no longer fires an energy arrow, it now fires a barrage of four glowing radiant arrows that ignore tiles, and explode shortly after contacting an enemy per use. However, radiant arrows can also pierce one enemy, which will cause the arrows to explode immediately after a second enemy is hit. Now requires and consumes four arrows per use. Buffed base damage from 70 to 176. Radiant arrows now remove enemy immunity frames on hit instead of granting enemies 1 local immunity frame.
*Reworked [[Vehemence]], it no longer fires a projectile with a 500,000 damage cap and inflicts several debuffs. While casted, it now fires out hellish bolts that explode into skulls that do 8% of the weapon's damage. Hellish beams inflict the Demon Flames debuff for 30 seconds on hit. Buffed base damage from 7 to 5185, its mana cost from 590 to 41, and its use time from 50 to 43.
*Reworked [[Void Vortex]], it no longer fires a spread of magnetic orbs that spin out over time. Now summons 12 supercharged magnet spheres in a circular pattern that travel clockwise and eventually fade out. Supercharged magnet spheres will fire beams at nearby enemies. Nerfed base damage from 195 to 140 and its use time from 41 to 80.
*Reworked [[Wrathwing]], the spear now trails fireballs as it travels. Stealth strikes spawn 13 cinders on hit instead of 3 fireballs, along with the regular strike effect. Nerfed base damage from 1000 to 592. Decreased stealth strike damage by 33%.
*Reworked Yharon's Kindle Staff, removed bonus defense, life regeneration, and movement speed while a Son of Yharon minion is summoned. Son of Yharon minions now bounce off of their targets and fire homing fireballs, instead of ramming back and forth through targets. Bounces deal 200% damage and create a small explosion. Increased its minion slot requirement from 4 to 5. Buffed base damage from 130 to 340.

=== Additional Changes ===
<!-- AC:A -->
*Adjusted the tooltip of [[The Absorber]] and [[The Sponge]] to clarify the amount of healing provided on hit.
*The [[Adrenaline Meter]], Rage Meter, and the Stealth Bar sizes can now be adjusted with the UI scaling slider in the Settings menu.
*The Adrenaline and Rage Meters now have UI indicators for their corresponding upgrades.
*The Adrenaline and Rage Meters will no longer disrupt item usage when hovered over with the cursor.
*All players now have a permanent 50% increase to tile placement speed.
*All projectiles spawned by weapons now ignore water physics. This does not include projectiles that are destroyed on contact with liquids.
*[[Ashes of Annihilation]] no longer float when dropped. It now uses the {{rare|15}} rarity instead of {{rare|16}}. <!-- also got a flavor tooltip -->
*Astral Chunk, Death Whistle, and Decapodita Sprout now spawn their respective bosses with an increased offset to prevent cheap hits. <!-- Not in the individual bosses sections because bosses can still spawn naturally -->
*Decreased Auric Ore pickaxe power requirement from 275% to 250%.
<!-- AC:B -->
*[[Blood Pact]]'s [[Bloody Boost]] buff can now be manually canceled.
*[[Bloodfin]]s, [[Hadal Stew]], and [[Margarita]] will no longer give the player Potion Sickness if they are consumed with full HP by {{key|Quick Heal}}.
*The [[Boss Health Bar]] will now glow red while a boss is enraged.
*All Boss Summoning items can no longer be used while the Boss Rush event is active.
*Most Boss Summoning items are now non-consumable, excluding [[Ashes of Calamity]], [[Bloodworm]]s, [[Lihzahrd Power Cell]]s, [[Titan Heart]]s, and [[Truffle Worm]]s. <!-- All non-consumable boss summons were given a "Not consumable" line on their tooltips. - Merkalto -->
**Decreased all non-consumable Boss Summoning items' stack limits from 20 to 1.
**All [[Town NPCs]] no longer sell Boss Summoning items except the [[Sea King]], who still sells Truffle Worms and Bloodworms. <!-- Calamity summons are also no longer sold by the Mutant NPC from [https://forums.terraria.org/index.php?threads/fargos-mutant-mod-summons-and-souls.42291/ Fargo's Mutant Mod]. -->
**The Sea King will now sell Truffle Worms for {{gc|10}} instead of {{gc|40}} and Bloodworms for {{pc|2}} instead of {{pc|8}}.
*Bound Town NPCs are now immune to all damage.
*Lava in the [[Brimstone Crag]] now has a pink-red color.
*Bullets now spawn dust 10 frames after they are spawned.
**[[Terra Bullet]] dust code redone to prevent frame drops.
<!-- AC:C -->
*[[Calamitas' Brew]] now affects both melee and Spy weapons.
*The [[Celestial Sigil]] will now instantly summon the Moon Lord.
*The Celestial Pillars event now permanently requires 100 enemies to be defeated per pillar. Pillars will now spawn enemies depending on how much HP the Pillar Shield has. A more detailed list can be found below:
{{collapse top|Celestial Pillar Enemy Tiers (Click to open)|(Click to close)|}}
*The following enemies will spawn at their corresponding pillar when the Pillar Shield is between 100% and 76% HP (Between 0 and 24 enemies defeated):
**The Solar Pillar will spawn [[Drakanian]]s, [[Drakomire]]s, [[Drakomire Rider]]s, and [[Sroller]]s.
**The Vortex Pillar will spawn [[Alien Larva|Alien Larvae]], [[Alien Hornet]]s, and [[Alien Queen]]s.
**The Nebula Pillar will spawn [[Brain Suckler]]s.
**The Stardust Pillar will spawn [[Milkyway Weaver]]s and [[Star Cell]]s.
*The following enemies will spawn at their corresponding pillar when the Pillar Shield is between 75% and 51% HP (Between 25 and 49 enemies defeated):
**The Solar Pillar will spawn Drakanians, Drakomire Riders, and Srollers.
**The Vortex Pillar will spawn Alien Larvae, Alien Hornets, Alien Queens, and [[Vortexian]]s.
**The Nebula Pillar will spawn Brain Sucklers and [[Predictor]]s.
**The Stardust Pillar will spawn Milkyway Weavers, [[Stargazer]]s, and [[Twinkle Popper]]s.
*The following enemies will spawn at their corresponding pillar when the Pillar Shield is between 50% and 26% HP (Between 50 and 74 enemies defeated):
**The Solar Pillar will spawn Drakanians, [[Selenian]]s, and Srollers.
**The Vortex Pillar will spawn Alien Larvae, Alien Hornets, Alien Queens, and [[Storm Diver]]s.
**The Nebula Pillar will spawn Brain Sucklers, Predictors, and [[Evolution Beast]]s.
**The Stardust Pillar will spawn Stargazers, Twinkle Poppers, and [[Flow Invader]]s.
*The following enemies will spawn at their corresponding pillar when the Pillar Shield is between 25% and 1% HP (Between 75 and 99 enemies defeated):
**The Solar Pillar will spawn [[Corite]]s, [[Crawltipede]]s, Selenians, and Srollers.
**The Vortex Pillar will spawn Alien Larvae, Alien Hornets, Alien Queens, and Storm Divers.
**The Nebula Pillar will spawn Brain Sucklers, Predictors, and Evolution Beasts.
**The Stardust Pillar will spawn Stargazers, Twinkle Poppers, and Flow Invaders.
*The following enemies will spawn at their corresponding pillar when the Pillar Shield has been broken:
**The Solar Pillar will spawn Corites, Crawltipedes, and Selenians.
**The Vortex Pillar will spawn Alien Larvae, Alien Hornets, Alien Queens, Vortexians, and Storm Divers.
**The Nebula Pillar will spawn Predictors, Evolution Beasts, and [[Nebula Floater]]s.
**The Stardust Pillar will spawn Twinkle Poppers and Flow Invaders.
{{collapse bottom}}
*[[Charred Ore]] no longer requires the Brimstone Elemental to be defeated for it to be mineable. Decreased its minimum pickaxe power requirement from 180% to 150%.
*Weapons boosted by Cinnamon Roll, [[Eskimo armor]], Evergreen Gin, and Fireball now have {{color|-  Fire Weapon - |#ffa500}}, {{color|- Ice Weapon - |#5ee6ff}}, and {{color|- Nature Weapon - |#2ea500}} tags respectively.
*Increased how fast [[Chlorophyte Ore]] can spread by 50% and how much ore can be generated in a world by 53.85%.
*[[Colossal Squid]]s can now spawn in the 4th layer of the Abyss.
*Corruption Effigy now reduces DR by 5% instead of 10%.
*The [[Cosmic Plushie]] will now ignore all player immunity frames when it stabs the player.
*The [[Cosmic Worm]], Jungle Dragon Egg, and the [[Eye of Extinction]] no longer require a boss to be defeated to use them.

*All item [[Rarity|rarities]] have been re-tiered for post-Moon Lord items to better indicate the boss tiers: <!-- All of these items will be ordered via progression instead of alphabetical, no I am not changing it nor am I listing EVERY ITEM. -Merkalto -->
**All Calamity items that are obtained from the following use {{rare|10}} rarity:
***Items crafted from either [[Luminite Bar]]s or any of the Lunar Fragments and any remaining items that can be crafted at an Ancient Manipulator that can be obtained before defeating the Moon Lord.
**All Calamity items that are obtained from the following will use the {{rare|11}} rarity:
***Post-Moon Lord items crafted from [[Galactica Singularity|Galactica Singularities]], Profaned Guardians drops, The Dragonfolly drops, any remaining items that can be obtained after defeating the Moon Lord but before defeating Providence, the Profaned Goddess.
****Rarity 11 items now have a base sell price of {{gc|22}}.
**All Calamity items that are obtained from the following will use the {{rare|12}} rarity:
***Providence, the Profaned Goddess drops, items crafted from [[Bloodstone Core]]s, [[Divine Geode]]s, and/or [[Uelibloom Bar]]s, [[Sentinels of the Devourer]] drops, any remaining items that can be obtained after defeating Providence but before defeating Polterghast.
**All Calamity items that are obtained from the following will use the {{rare|13}} rarity:
***Polterghast drops, items that are crafted using [[Ruinous Soul]]s, Acid Rain Tier 3 drops, The Old Duke drops, and any remaining items that can be obtained after defeating Polterghast but before defeating The Devourer of Gods.
**All Calamity items that are obtained from the following will use the {{rare|14}} rarity:
***The Devourer of Gods drops, items that are crafted using either [[Ascendant Spirit Essence]] or any of its materials, and/or [[Cosmilite Bar]]s, and any remaining items that can be obtained after defeating The Devourer of Gods but before defeating the Jungle Dragon, Yharon.
**All Calamity items that are obtained from the following will use the {{rare|15}} rarity:
***Draedon and Exo Mech drops, Jungle Dragon, Yharon drops, Supreme Calamitas drops, items crafted with Ashes of Annihilation, Auric Bars, Exo Prisms, Yharon Soul Fragments, and any remaining items that can be crafted after defeating the Jungle Dragon, Yharon.
**All Calamity [[Donor items]] now have their corresponding rarity displayed in their tooltip. In order to still allow for donor item identification, all donor items now have a {{color|- Donor Item -|#8b0000}} ({{color|#8b0000 ●|#8b0000}}) tag at the bottom of their tooltips.
**All Calamity [[Developer items]] now have a {{color|- Developer Item -|#ff00ff}} ({{color|#ff00ff ●|#ff00ff}}) tag at the bottom of their tooltips.
**All post-Moon Lord rarities now internally default to {{rare|11}} internally instead of {{rare|10}}. <!-- This has no real effect on gameplay. -->
*The tooltip of the [[Revengeance]] item has been updated to match the new effects of Rage Mode. <!-- Also capitalized the "A" in Adrenaline. -->
*The following Revengeance Mode changes now always apply to all worlds:
**[[Cursed Inferno]] now decreases enemy and player damage reduction by 20% while inflicted.
**The Nurse's heal-price will now scale with progression.
**Life regeneration now scales from 50% effectiveness at full HP to 100% effectiveness at 1 HP.
<!-- AC:S -->
*All sound effect volumes have been capped.
*Sparkling Empress can now only be fished up once the Desert Scourge has been defeated.
*The [[Stat Meter]] now has dynamic defense and damage reduction information depending on if the player is affected by Defense Damage.
*The Stat Meter can now be purchased from the Goblin Tinkerer for {{gc|50}} instead of {{pc|1}}.
*The Steampunker NPC is no longer forced to spawn once the Wall of Flesh has been defeated.
*The [[Stylist]] Town NPC sells the Stylish Scissors for {{gc|15}}.
*Expanded the tooltips of [[Sulphurous armor]] and [[Victide armor]] for more clearance.
<!-- AC:T -->
*Tooltip for true melee damage now only appears on true melee weapons.
*The Microwave kill sound now has an internal 0.75 second cooldown.
*Slightly reworded Torrential Tear's tooltip.
*Several Town NPCs now sell potions:
{{collapse top|Town NPC potion shops (Click to Open)|(Click to Close}}
*The [[Archmage]] can now sell Warmth Potions for {{gc|2}}.
*The [[Arms Dealer]] can now sell Ammo Reservation Potions for {{gc|1}} and Hunter Potions for {{gc|2}}.
**The Arms Dealer can also sell Battle Potions for {{gc|2}} once either the Eater of Worlds or Brain of Cthulhu has been defeated.
*The [[Bandit]] can now sell Invisibility, Night Owl, and Dangersense Potions for {{gc|1}} each.
*The [[Demolitionist]] can now sell Mining, Ironskin, and Shine Potions for {{gc|1}} once the Eye of Cthulhu has been defeated.
**The Demolitionist can also sell Obsidian Skin Potions for {{gc|2}} and Endurance Potions for {{gc|2}} in Pre-Hardmode, {{gc|4}} in Hardmode, and {{gc|8}} post-Moon Lord.
**The Demolitionist can also sell Spelunker Potions for {{gc|2}} once either the Eater of Worlds or Brain of Cthulhu has been defeated.
*The [[Drunk Princess]] can now sell Heartreach Potions for {{gc|2}} and Love Potions for {{gc|1}}.
**The Drunk Princess can also sell Lifeforce Potions for {{gc|8}} once the Moon Lord has been defeated.
*The [[Dryad]] can now sell Thorns and Featherfall Potions for {{gc|1}} each.
**The Dryad can also sell Regeneration Potions for {{gc|2}} and Swiftness Potions for {{gc|2}} in Pre-Hardmode, {{gc|4}} in Hardmode, and {{gc|8}} post-Moon Lord once either the Eater of Worlds or Brain of Cthulhu has been defeated.
*The [[Goblin Tinkerer]] can now sell Stink Potions for {{gc|1}}.
*The [[Mechanic]] can now sell Builder Potions for {{gc|1}}.
*The [[Merchant]] can now sell Bottles for {{cc|20}} and Archery Potions for {{gc|2}} in Pre-Hardmode, {{gc|4}} in Hardmode, and {{gc|8}} post-Moon Lord.
**The Merchant can also sell Healing, Mana, and Titan Potions for {{sc|10}}, {{sc|2}} {{cc|50}}, and {{gc|2}} respectively.
**The Merchant can also sell Wormhole Potions for {{sc|25}}.
*The [[Party Girl]] can now sell Gender Change Potions for {{gc|1}}.
*The [[Sea King]] can now sell Gills, Water Walking and Flipper Potions for {{gc|1}} each.
*The [[Skeleton Merchant]] can now sell Calcium Potions for {{gc|1}}.
*The Witch Doctor can now sell Calming Potions for {{gc|1}} and Inferno Potions for {{gc|2}}.
**The Witch Doctor can also sell Rage and Wrath Potions for {{gc|2}} in Pre-Hardmode, {{gc|4}} in Hardmode, and {{gc|8}} post-Moon Lord each.
*The [[Wizard]] can now sell Mana Regeneration and Magic Power Potions for {{gc|2}} in Pre-Hardmode, {{gc|4}} in Hardmode, and {{gc|8}} post-Moon Lord each.
**The Wizard can also sell Gravitation Potions for {{gc|2}}.

== Fixes ==
=== Gameplay ===
<!-- FIXG:A -->
*Fixed a bug that allowed [[Normality Relocator]] to be used while the player was inflicted with the Frozen, Webbed, or Slowed debuffs.
*Fixed a bug that allowed [[Ravager]] to fall infinitely through platforms in its second phase slam.
*Fixed a bug that allowed [[Sulphuric Poisoning]] to be inflicted on the player while they were submerged in Honey or Lava.
*Fixed a bug that caused armor penetration to incorrectly calculate its bonus damage on enemies that are affected by DR decreasing debuffs.
*Fixed a bug that caused Draedon Arsenal weapons to lose their [[Charge]] when reforged.
*Fixed a bug that caused The Dragonfolly to become permanently invulnerable if its second phase was skipped.
*Fixed a bug that caused [[Gilded Dagger|Gilded]] and [[Gleaming Dagger]] projectiles to linger for extended periods of time when killing an enemy with a stealth strike.
*Fixed a bug that caused Phaseslayer to fire projectiles faster when angled at -π.
*Fixed a bug that caused Phaseslayer to skip one of the frames of the player's swing animation when facing left.
*Fixed a bug that prevented Eternity from auto-targeting another enemy after killing its first target.
*Fixed a bug that prevented [[Hermit's Box of One Hundred Medicines]] from healing the player to full HP when respawning as intended.
*Fixed a bug where the Cobalt Kunai, Dune Hopper, and Stellar Knife stealth strike projectiles could not proc the effects of certain Spy coverings.
*Fixed a bug where Death Mode Lava Geysers in the Brimstone Crag did not deal damage to players.
*Fixed a bug where the raining projectiles spawned from the Venerated Locket were counted as stealth strike projectiles for the Duke's Decapitator, Luminous Striker, and Scarlet Devil.
*Fixed a bug where the Squirrel Squire minion would be able to get stuck around blocks and slopes.
*Fixed a damage calculation error with The Dragonfolly's lightning projectile. <!-- Yes, this was an issue again. -->
*Fixed a desync issue that caused [[Wulfrum Gyrator]]s to teleport while jumping.
*Fixed a desync issue that prevented [[Super Dummy|Super Dummies]] from despawning.
*Fixed a rare bug where hovering over a Calamity [[Chests|Chest]] via the map would cause a game crash.
*Fixed a vanilla exploit that allowed the Brand of the Inferno [[Striking Moment]] buff to transfer over to other weapons.
<!-- FIXG:AN -->
*Fixed an exploit involving velocity multipliers being given to the player while wearing the Snow Ruffian armor while in minecarts.
**Additionally, fixed an exploit that allowed players to glide with the Snow Ruffian armor while in minecarts.
*Fixed an exploit that allowed players to deal extreme amounts of damage against Astrum Deus using Yanmei's Knife.
*Fixed an exploit that allowed players to gain two dodges from swapping the Abyssal Mirror and Eclipse Mirror when one or the other's dodge was on cooldown.
*Fixed an exploit that allowed stealth strike projectiles to multiply per use when using certain Spy weapons.
*Fixed an issue that allowed bosses to spawn naturally while other bosses are alive in Death Mode.
*Fixed an issue that allowed certain projectiles to hit Town NPCs.
*Fixed an issue that allowed the Gauss Dagger aura damage to scale exponentially.
*Fixed an issue that caused [[Bloodfire Bullet]]s and the [[True Bloody Edge]] to incorrectly function during PvP.
*Fixed an issue that caused chunk-loading errors when entering a biome added by the Calamity Mod in multiplayer.
*Fixed an issue that caused Cosmic Guardian's audio cue to be extremely loud.
*Fixed an issue that caused [[Plague Mine]] hitboxes to levitate above their sprite.
*Fixed an issue that caused Spy Stealth to not properly reset when using a Spy weapon without max-stealth.
*Fixed an issue that caused the [[Crystyl Crusher]] and Sparkling Empress beams to break or get stuck in the air.
*Fixed an issue that caused the player to be hit twice while performing a shield bash or bounce.
*Fixed an issue that caused The Devourer of Gods' phase 2 music to be out of sync by 10 frames.
*Fixed an issue that caused the game to crash if the player loaded in an Arsenal Lab Turret without a dedicated graphics card installed on their PC.
*Fixed an issue that caused the game to lag or crash when Slime God Core drops Gel upon its defeat.
*Fixed an issue that caused the Orichalcum Spiked Gemstone stealth strike to enable all sequential uses of the weapon to be stealth strikes regardless if the player has their Stealth Meter full.
*Fixed an issue that caused the Tactical Plague Engine bullet damage to scale several times in its damage calculation.
*Fixed an issue that occasionally prevented Signus, Envoy of the Devourer from spawning during the Sentinels phase of The Devourer of Gods fight.
*Fixed an issue that prevented players from performing two stealth strikes consecutively with the Coin of Deceit line of coverings.
*Fixed an issue that prevented the [[Latcher Mine]] fire projectiles from dealing damage.
*Fixed an issue with the Relic of Deliverance that allowed players to clip into tiles.
*Fixed an oversight that allowed Ark of the Ancients and True Ark of the Ancients' Eon Beam projectiles to pierce infinitely.
*Fixed an oversight that allowed Feather Knives to duplicate when performing a stealth strike.
*Fixed an oversight that allowed Gael's Greatsword lightning projectile dealing flat damage and not scaling with melee damage bonuses.
*Fixed an oversight that allowed Plaguenade stealth strikes to throw out two grenades instead of one.
*Fixed an oversight that allowed players to receive additional bonuses from the Warbanner of the Sun when nearby enemy segments.
**Fixed an additional oversight that caused the Warbanner of the Sun to measure its enemy bonuses from the center of enemy hitboxes instead of the sides of the hitbox.
*Fixed an oversight that allowed the Statigel and Sulphurous armor double jumps to proc while the player was on a mount that disabled double jumps.
*Fixed an oversight that allowed [[Water Leech]]s to inherit contact damage from the enemy that hit you last.
*Fixed an oversight that caused the [[Mounted Scanner]] to exponentially increase its minion damage while losing charge.
*Fixed an oversight where the [[Lazure's Barrier Platform]] was not a part of the "Any Wings" crafting group.
*Fixed an oversight where the [[Monolith Amalgam]] counted as a [[bookcase]].
*Statigel and Sulphurous armor double jumps now properly proc while the player is on a mount that has double jumps enabled.
*Fixed an oversight where Time Bolt non-stealth strike projectiles had more enemy penetration than intended.
*Fixed an oversight where Web Ball stealth strike projectiles had flat damage.
<!-- FIXG:Rest -->
*Fixed buy and sell prices being exaggerated in the code.
*Fixed Desecrated Water, Pwnagehammer, and Vivid Clarity dealing damage to Town NPCs.
*Fixed despawn issues for worm-based segmented enemies and bosses.
*Fixed Frostcrush Valari not firing as many icicles as intended on stealth strikes.
*Fixed Gael's Greatsword small skull projectiles moving faster than intended.
*Fixed Golem (free head) hitbox being significantly lower than its sprite.
*Fixed incorrect sell price values for the following items:
**Alpha Virus, [[The Evolution]], [[Pulse Turret Remote]], Titan Arm, [[Ursa Sergeant]].
*Fixed Jaws of Oblivion stealth strike projectiles doing significantly less damage than intended.
*Fixed several instances where immunity frame bonuses from items would not be granted.
*Fixed several desync issues with the Providence, the Profaned Goddess, Ravager, and Wall of Flesh boss fights.
*Fixed several issues with [[Furniture]] not properly counting towards Town NPC housing, crafting recipes, and light sources.
*Fixed the hitboxes of Aquashard Shotgun shard being larger than its actual sprite.
*Fixed the hitboxes of The Devourer of Gods' fireballs, The Plaguebringer Goliath's hive bomb, Ravager's homing dart, and Yharon's fireballs being smaller than its actual sprite.
*Fixed various Aquatic Scourge despawning issues. <!-- Now despawns at 800 blocks away. -->
*Fixed various bugs where the game would crash if the player was too far away from a boss when a background shader was changed.
*Fixed various issues with the Counter Scarf, [[Mirage Mirror]] line, Evasion Scarf, and Spectral Veil dodges.
*Fixed [[Verium Bullet]]s being able to target friendly NPCs.

=== Visual ===
<!-- FIXV:A -->
*All tooltips that use an ellipse now have proper spacing and capitalization.
*Fixed a rendering error with debuff icons that appear on top of The Hive Mind's second phase.
*Fixed a spelling error of ''a'' instead of ''an'' in [https://forums.terraria.org/index.php?threads/boss-checklist-in-game-progression-checklist.50668/ Boss Checklist] Mod's instruction to summon Ravager.
*Fixed a sprite exportation oversight where small Astral ambient objects had their vanilla counterpart's sprite layered beneath them.
*Fixed [[Abyss Dresser]] and [[Profaned Dresser]] having incorrectly cropped highlights.
<!-- FIXV:AN -->
*Fixed an issue where Anahita Mask, Anahita Trophy, Calamitas Mask, Lead Wizard, and [[Ancient God Slayer armor]] were missing on Boss Checklist Mod's boss log function.
*Fixed an issue with stalactites that generate in the Sulphurous Sea where it would disconnect or cut off.
*Fixed an issue with The Dance of Light where its projectiles would rapidly flash in multiplayer.
*Fixed an issue with Event Horizon black hole projectiles incorrectly displaying on enemies.
*Fixed an issue with Nanotech incorrectly displaying crit levels.
*Fixed an issue that caused Nuclear Fury's projectiles to incorrectly draw and display only a third of the projectile sprite.
*Fixed an issue that prevented all backpack-like equipped sprites from drawing and displaying.
*Fixed an issue that prevented [[Ancient Lantern]], [[Ancient Chandelier]], and [[Profaned Chandelier]] from turning off using wires.
<!-- FIXV:Rest -->
*Fixed Asteroid Staff's holding animation being incorrectly offset.
*Fixed Blunder Booster, [[Fire Gauntlet]], and Plagued Fuel Pack's outdated tooltips, they now reflect their current functions.
*Fixed Eclipse Mirror explosion drawing issues.
*Fixed Epidemic Shredder missing ''to'' in its tooltip.
*Fixed Fantasy Talisman souls facing the wrong direction when thrown to the left during a stealth strike.
*Fixed Gravistar Sabaton's spelling error of ''a'' instead of ''an'' in its tooltip.
*Fixed Hydrothermic Mask not listing its +100 mana bonus in the tooltip while Death Mode was enabled.
*Fixed [[Profaned Dresser]]s having an inconsistent and outdated highlight.
*Fixed Relic of Deliverance tooltip saying "more strong" instead of "stronger".
*Fixed Saros Possession minion giving off too much light when idle.
*Fixed sprite rotation issues with the following:
**Aetherflux Cannon's holdout projectile, Contagion's holdout projectile, Crystal Piercer's thrown projectile, Frosty Flare's thrown projectile, Hiveling (enemy), Lionfish's thrown projectile, and Nano Purge's holdout projectile.
*Fixed System Bane and The Syringe stealth strikes incorrectly drawing.
*Fixed The Devourer of Gods' sky shaders displaying out of sync or at the incorrect AI phase.
*Fixed sprite sheeting errors with the following:
**[[Aquatic Scourge Trophy]], [[Aries]], [[Goldplume Spear]] projectile, [[Hive (enemy)]], Hive Pod, [[Profaned Bench]], Profaned Chandelier, [[Profaned Piano]]. <!-- AS trophy fix is technically a respite -->
*Fixed [[Ultima]] being held slightly off center.
*Removed a redundant line on the [[Plague Hive]]'s tooltip.
*Removed a stray white pixel on The Leviathan's sprite. <!-- Thanks YuH! -->
*Reverted an accidental change that replaced the [[Ancient Ice Chunk]]'s Ice Clasper minion's unique sprite with the enemy's sprite.
<!-- Also Fab removed a bunch of oxford commas for whatever reason, but not all of them. - Merkalto -->

=== Miscellaneous ===
*Cleaned up several internal utilities to prevent potential issues.
*Corrected an oversight where "Empyrean" was not a viable string for the <code>GetSetBonus</code> mod call for Empyrean armor.
*Fixed potential out-of-bounds crashes due to the projectile limit.

*Removed [[Daylight]], [[Determination Breaker]], [[Enchanted Metal]], Frigid Bar, [[Holiday Halberd]], [[Moonlight]], Profaned Core (consumable), [[Reaver Helmet]], and [[Reaver Mask]].
*Removed Absolute Rage, Evasion Scarf Boost, and Scarf Boost.
<!-- *Removed the first phase of [[Calamitas]]. Technically a separate NPC with its own textures -->
*Removed the [[Any Seasonal Energy]] item grouping.
*Removed the {{rare|d}} and {{rare|riv}} rarities.
*Removed the <code>BossRushXerocCurse</code>, <code>FullPowerReactiveBossDR</code>, <code>MiningSpeedBoost</code>, <code>NerfExpertPillars</code>, <code>Rippers</code>, and <code>SellVanillaSummons</code> configuration options.
